```ts
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Design spec, "How it is built": every change is one named command; the engine solves after each;
// a drag previews and records once (ADR 015, rule 2). Numbers: Operation specs, bookcase.

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { setSize, startProject } from "./commands";
import { createStorage } from "./storage";
import { createKerfStore, shownProject } from "./store";
import { memoryStorage } from "../testing/memory-storage";

const fresh = () => createKerfStore({ storage: createStorage(memoryStorage()) });
const shelfCut = (s: ReturnType<typeof fresh>) => s.getState().solved?.result.cutList.lines.find((l) => l.parts.some((p) => p.name === "Shelf"))?.length;

describe("createKerfStore()", () => {
  it("starts with no project, on the Start step", () => {
    const s = fresh().getState();
    expect(s.history).toBeNull();
    expect(s.step).toBe("Start");
  });

  it("solves the bookcase as soon as it is started: shelves 35-1/16 cut", () => {
    const s = fresh();
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    expect(shelfCut(s)).toBe(7_124_700);
    expect(s.getState().solved?.assembly).toHaveLength(4);
  });

  it("records Width 36\" → 30\" as one entry and re-solves: shelves 29-1/16 cut", () => {
    const s = fresh();
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    const p = shownProject(s.getState());
    if (!p) throw new Error("no project");
    const r = setSize(p, p.structures[0]?.id ?? "", "w", '30"');
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.message);
    s.getState().apply(r.edit);
    expect(s.getState().history?.entries.map((e) => e.label)).toEqual(["New bookcase", 'Width 36" → 30"']);
    expect(shelfCut(s)).toBe(5_905_500);
  });

  it("undoes back to 36\" and re-solves", () => {
    const s = fresh();
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    const p = shownProject(s.getState());
    const r = p && setSize(p, p.structures[0]?.id ?? "", "w", '30"');
    if (!r?.ok) throw new Error("resize failed");
    s.getState().apply(r.edit);
    s.getState().undo();
    expect(shelfCut(s)).toBe(7_124_700);
  });

  it("records one entry for a drag of 200 previews, ending exactly on the last value", () => {
    const s = fresh();
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    const base = shownProject(s.getState());
    if (!base) throw new Error("no project");
    const id = base.structures[0]?.id ?? "";
    let last = base;
    for (let step = 1; step <= 200; step += 1) {
      // 36" minus 200 steps of 1/64" = 36 − 3-1/8 = 32-7/8".
      const r = setSize(base, id, "w", `36in - ${step}in/64`);
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.message);
      last = r.edit.project;
      s.getState().preview(last);
    }
    s.getState().commit('Width 36" → 32-7/8"');
    expect(s.getState().history?.entries).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(shownProject(s.getState())).toEqual(last);
    // 32-7/8 − 2 × 23/32 + 2 × 1/4 = 31-15/16" = 31 × 203200 + 15/16 × 203200 = 6489700.
    expect(shelfCut(s)).toBe(6_489_700);
  });

  it("cancels a preview and shows the recorded project again", () => {
    const s = fresh();
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    const base = shownProject(s.getState());
    const r = base && setSize(base, base.structures[0]?.id ?? "", "w", '30"');
    if (!r?.ok) throw new Error("resize failed");
    s.getState().preview(r.edit.project);
    s.getState().cancelPreview();
    expect(shelfCut(s)).toBe(7_124_700);
    expect(s.getState().history?.entries).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("shows a notice with the change when one is given", () => {
    const s = fresh();
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"), "Shelf 3 is now custom; the form won't change it");
    expect(s.getState().notice).toEqual({ text: "Shelf 3 is now custom; the form won't change it", undo: true });
  });
});
```

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The five workflow steps (Interaction spec, "Workflow modules") and the one-line hint each shows
// under the top bar (design spec, "Layout").

import type { Structure } from "@kerf/core";

export const STEPS = ["Start", "Shape", "Divide", "Joinery", "Build plan"] as const;
export type Step = (typeof STEPS)[number];

/** What the step is for, in one sentence. */
export function hintFor(step: Step, type: Structure["type"] | undefined): string {
  const piece = type === "box" ? "box" : "case";
  switch (step) {
    case "Start":
      return "Start — pick a template and set the overall size.";
    case "Shape":
      return `Shape — choose how the ${piece} is built. Every board follows the form until you edit it by hand.`;
    case "Divide":
      return "Divide — click an opening, then add shelves, a partition or bays.";
    case "Joinery":
      return "Joinery — check the joints your tools can cut, then accept them.";
    case "Build plan":
      return "Build plan — what to buy, what to cut, and in what order.";
  }
}
```

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// What the user has picked (Interaction spec, "Select": parts, openings). Boards are placed copies
// (`PlacedPart.instance`), so one shelf of a row can be picked on its own.

import type { Id } from "@kerf/core";

export type Selection =
  | { kind: "none" }
  | { kind: "structure"; structure: Id }
  | { kind: "opening"; structure: Id; opening: Id }
  | { kind: "boards"; instances: Id[] };

export const NOTHING: Selection = { kind: "none" };
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `model/store.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The app's state (design spec, "How it is built", layer 2): the project as History, a preview
// while something is dragged, the solve of whatever is shown, and the step, selection, shop and
// tool. Every change goes through `apply` (or `preview` then `commit`), and the engine solves the
// shown project straight after, so the screens, the cut list and the file can never disagree.

import { type AssemblyStep, assemblyOrder, type Project, type ShopProfile, type SolveResult, solve } from "@kerf/core";
import { createStore, type StoreApi } from "zustand/vanilla";
import type { Edit } from "./commands";
import { currentProject, type History, jumpTo, record, redo, startHistory, undo } from "./history";
import { NOTHING, type Selection } from "./selection";
import { loadShop, saveShop } from "./shop";
import type { Step } from "./steps";
import type { AppStorage } from "./storage";

export interface Solved {
  result: SolveResult;
  assembly: AssemblyStep[];
  /** How long the solve took, for the speed budget (Charter question 3). */
  ms: number;
}

export interface Notice {
  text: string;
  /** True when the notice offers Undo for the change it describes. */
  undo: boolean;
}

export type ToolId = "select" | "board" | "pushPull" | "move" | "join" | "edge" | "tape";

export interface KerfState {
  history: History | null;
  preview: Project | null;
  solved: Solved | null;
  step: Step;
  selection: Selection;
  shop: ShopProfile;
  notice: Notice | null;
  tool: ToolId;
  theme: "dark" | "light";
}

export interface KerfActions {
  /** Records a change; `notice` is shown with Undo (for example after a board is taken over). */
  apply(edit: Edit, notice?: string): void;
  /** Shows a project without recording it (while dragging). */
  preview(project: Project): void;
  /** Records the previewed project as one change. */
  commit(label: string): void;
  cancelPreview(): void;
  undo(): void;
  redo(): void;
  jumpTo(index: number): void;
  setStep(step: Step): void;
  select(selection: Selection): void;
  setShop(shop: ShopProfile): void;
  setTool(tool: ToolId): void;
  setTheme(theme: "dark" | "light"): void;
  say(text: string, undo?: boolean): void;
  dismissNotice(): void;
}

export type KerfStore = StoreApi<KerfState & KerfActions>;

/** The project on screen: the preview while dragging, otherwise History's current entry. */
export function shownProject(state: KerfState): Project | null {
  return state.preview ?? (state.history ? currentProject(state.history) : null);
}

export function createKerfStore(deps: { storage: AppStorage; clock?: () => number }): KerfStore {
  const clock = deps.clock ?? (() => performance.now());
  const loaded = loadShop(deps.storage);

  const solveFor = (project: Project | null, shop: ShopProfile): Solved | null => {
    if (!project) return null;
    const started = clock();
    const result = solve(project, shop);
    const assembly = assemblyOrder(project, shop);
    return { result, assembly, ms: clock() - started };
  };

  return createStore<KerfState & KerfActions>()((set, get) => {
    /** Sets new state and re-solves what is shown. */
    const show = (patch: Partial<KerfState>): void => {
      const next = { ...get(), ...patch };
      set({ ...patch, solved: solveFor(shownProject(next), next.shop) });
    };

    return {
      history: null,
      preview: null,
      solved: null,
      step: "Start",
      selection: NOTHING,
      shop: loaded.shop,
      notice: loaded.problem ? { text: loaded.problem, undo: false } : null,
      tool: "select",
      theme: deps.storage.read("kerf.theme") === "light" ? "light" : "dark",

      apply: (edit, notice) => {
        const h = get().history;
        show({
          history: h ? record(h, edit) : startHistory(edit),
          preview: null,
          notice: notice ? { text: notice, undo: true } : get().notice,
        });
      },
      preview: (project) => show({ preview: project }),
      commit: (label) => {
        const { preview, history } = get();
        if (!preview) return;
        const edit = { label, project: preview };
        show({ history: history ? record(history, edit) : startHistory(edit), preview: null });
      },
      cancelPreview: () => show({ preview: null }),
      undo: () => {
        const h = get().history;
        if (h) show({ history: undo(h), preview: null, notice: null });
      },
      redo: () => {
        const h = get().history;
        if (h) show({ history: redo(h), preview: null });
      },
      jumpTo: (index) => {
        const h = get().history;
        if (h) show({ history: jumpTo(h, index), preview: null });
      },
      setStep: (step) => set({ step }),
      select: (selection) => set({ selection }),
      setShop: (shop) => {
        saveShop(deps.storage, shop);
        show({ shop });
      },
      setTool: (tool) => set({ tool }),
      setTheme: (theme) => {
        deps.storage.write("kerf.theme", theme);
        set({ theme });
      },
      say: (text, undoable = false) => set({ notice: { text, undo: undoable } }),
      dismissNotice: () => set({ notice: null }),
    };
  });
}
```

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Design spec, "Saving": the current project is kept in browser storage after every change, so a
// reload restores it; ADR 015, rule 11 for what happens when that cannot work.

import { serializeProject } from "@kerf/core";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { setUnits, startProject } from "./commands";
import { type AppStorage, createStorage } from "./storage";
import { createKerfStore, shownProject } from "./store";
import { memoryStorage } from "../testing/memory-storage";

const memory = (): AppStorage => createStorage(memoryStorage());

describe("the store across a reload", () => {
  it("brings back the last project, as one History entry", () => {
    const storage = memory();
    createKerfStore({ storage }).getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    const again = createKerfStore({ storage }).getState();
    expect(again.history?.entries.map((e) => e.label)).toEqual(["Opened your last project"]);
    expect(again.solved?.result.cutList.lines).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it("sets aside a stored project the engine rejects, says so, and starts empty", () => {
    const storage = memory();
    storage.write("kerf.project", '{"schemaVersion": 1, "name": 7}');
    const s = createKerfStore({ storage }).getState();
    expect(s.history).toBeNull();
    expect(s.notice?.text).toMatch(/^Your last project could not be opened/);
    expect(storage.read("kerf.project.setAside")).toBe('{"schemaVersion": 1, "name": 7}');
    expect(storage.read("kerf.project")).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("says once that work will not survive a reload when the browser has no storage", () => {
    const s = createKerfStore({ storage: createStorage(undefined) }).getState();
    expect(s.notice?.text).toBe("This browser won't keep your work between visits. Use Save to keep a copy.");
  });

  it("starts new projects in the units last chosen", () => {
    const storage = memory();
    const s = createKerfStore({ storage });
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    const p = shownProject(s.getState());
    if (!p) throw new Error("no project");
    s.getState().apply(setUnits(p, "metric"));
    expect(createKerfStore({ storage }).getState().unitsForNew()).toBe("metric");
  });

  it("writes the project exactly as the engine saves it", () => {
    const storage = memory();
    const s = createKerfStore({ storage });
    s.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    const p = shownProject(s.getState());
    if (!p) throw new Error("no project");
    expect(storage.read("kerf.project")).toBe(serializeProject(p));
  });
});
```

```ts
  /** The stored project, or why it could not be opened (it is then set aside, never lost). */
  const restore = (): { history: History | null; problem?: string } => {
    const text = deps.storage.read("kerf.project");
    if (text === undefined) return { history: null };
    try {
      return { history: startHistory({ label: "Opened your last project", project: parseProject(text) }) };
    } catch (error) {
      deps.storage.write("kerf.project.setAside", text);
      deps.storage.remove("kerf.project");
      const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "it was damaged";
      return { history: null, problem: `Your last project could not be opened (${reason}). It was set aside.` };
    }
  };
  const restored = restore();
  const firstNotice =
    loaded.problem ??
    restored.problem ??
    (deps.storage.available ? undefined : "This browser won't keep your work between visits. Use Save to keep a copy.");

  /** Keeps the shown History entry in storage and remembers its units. */
  const keep = (history: History | null): void => {
    if (!history) return;
    const project = currentProject(history);
    deps.storage.write("kerf.project", serializeProject(project));
    deps.storage.write("kerf.units", project.units);
  };
```

`packages/app/src/tokens.test.ts`:

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Design spec, "Design tokens": every text/background pair reads at 4.5:1 in both themes (WCAG 2.1
// contrast ratio: (L1 + 0.05) / (L2 + 0.05), relative luminance from sRGB).

import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const css = readFileSync(new URL("./tokens.css", import.meta.url), "utf8");

function theme(selector: string): Record<string, string> {
  const block = css.split(selector)[1]?.split("}")[0] ?? "";
  return Object.fromEntries([...block.matchAll(/--([\w-]+):\s*(#[0-9a-f]{6})/gi)].map((m) => [m[1] as string, m[2] as string]));
}

function luminance(hex: string): number {
  const [r, g, b] = [1, 3, 5].map((i) => {
    const c = Number.parseInt(hex.slice(i, i + 2), 16) / 255;
    return c <= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4;
  });
  return 0.2126 * (r ?? 0) + 0.7152 * (g ?? 0) + 0.0722 * (b ?? 0);
}

function contrast(a: string, b: string): number {
  const [hi, lo] = [luminance(a), luminance(b)].sort((x, y) => y - x);
  return ((hi ?? 0) + 0.05) / ((lo ?? 0) + 0.05);
}

const PAIRS: Array<[string, string]> = [
  ["text", "bg"], ["text", "surface"], ["text", "surface-raised"],
  ["text-muted", "bg"], ["text-muted", "surface"],
  ["accent", "surface"], ["error", "surface"], ["warning", "surface"],
  ["step-active-text", "accent"], ["text", "hover"],
];

describe.each([
  ["dark", ':root[data-theme="dark"] {'],
  ["light", ':root[data-theme="light"] {'],
])("the %s theme", (_name, selector) => {
  const t = theme(selector);
  it.each(PAIRS)("reads %s on %s at 4.5:1 or better", (fg, bg) => {
    expect(t[fg], `--color-${fg} missing`).toBeDefined();
    expect(t[bg], `--color-${bg} missing`).toBeDefined();
    expect(contrast(t[fg] as string, t[bg] as string)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
  });
});
```

(The `theme()` helper reads `--color-*` names without the `color-` prefix: write the regex as `/--color-([\w-]+):\s*(#[0-9a-f]{6})/gi`. The dark selector in `tokens.css` is `:root,\n:root[data-theme="dark"] {`; split on `:root[data-theme="dark"] {`.)

`packages/app/src/App.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Interaction spec, "Workflow modules" and "Screen layout"; design spec, "Layout".

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { App } from "./App";
import { startProject } from "./model/commands";
import { createStorage } from "./model/storage";
import { createKerfStore } from "./model/store";
import { memoryStorage } from "./testing/memory-storage";

function renderApp() {
  const store = createKerfStore({ storage: createStorage(memoryStorage()) });
  render(<App store={store} model={null} />);
  return store;
}

describe("<App>", () => {
  it("shows the five steps, Start first and pressed", () => {
    renderApp();
    const steps = screen.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Workflow" }).querySelectorAll("button");
    expect([...steps].map((b) => b.textContent)).toEqual(["Start", "Shape", "Divide", "Joinery", "Build plan"]);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Start" }).getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("true");
  });

  it("moves to Shape and shows what Shape is for", () => {
    renderApp();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Shape" }));
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Shape" }).getAttribute("aria-pressed")).toBe("true");
    expect(screen.getByText(/^Shape — choose how the case is built/)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("has the project, model and inspector regions", () => {
    renderApp();
    for (const name of ["Project", "Model", "Inspector"]) expect(screen.getByRole("region", { name }) ?? screen.getByLabelText(name)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("offers Undo only once there is something to undo", () => {
    const store = renderApp();
    expect((screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Undo" }) as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
    store.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    store.getState().apply(startProject("Blank case", "imperial"));
    expect((screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Undo" }) as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(false);
  });

  it("undoes with Ctrl+Z and redoes with Ctrl+Shift+Z", () => {
    const store = renderApp();
    store.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    store.getState().apply(startProject("Blank case", "imperial"));
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "z", ctrlKey: true });
    expect(store.getState().history?.at).toBe(0);
    fireEvent.keyDown(window, { key: "z", ctrlKey: true, shiftKey: true });
    expect(store.getState().history?.at).toBe(1);
  });

  it("shows a notice with an Undo this button that undoes the change", () => {
    const store = renderApp();
    store.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
    store.getState().apply(startProject("Blank case", "imperial"), "Shelf 3 is now custom; the form won't change it");
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toContain("Shelf 3 is now custom");
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Undo this" }));
    expect(store.getState().history?.at).toBe(0);
    expect(screen.queryByRole("status")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("puts the theme on the page", () => {
    renderApp();
    expect(document.documentElement.dataset.theme).toBe("dark");
  });
});
```

Also create `packages/app/src/testing/memory-storage.ts` if Task 9 has not (it is the shared in-memory `Storage` the model tests use):

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// An in-memory browser Storage for tests (a real one needs a browser). `failWrites` behaves like a
// full store, which throws QuotaExceededError on write.

export function memoryStorage(failWrites = false): Storage {
  const map = new Map<string, string>();
  return {
    get length() {
      return map.size;
    },
    clear: () => map.clear(),
    getItem: (k) => map.get(k) ?? null,
    key: (i) => [...map.keys()][i] ?? null,
    removeItem: (k) => {
      map.delete(k);
    },
    setItem: (k, v) => {
      if (failWrites) throw new DOMException("The store is full.", "QuotaExceededError");
      map.set(k, v);
    },
  };
}
```

`ui/useKerf.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// How screens reach the app's store (design spec, "How it is built", layer 3).

import { createContext, type ReactNode, useContext } from "react";
import { useStore } from "zustand";
import type { KerfActions, KerfState, KerfStore } from "../model/store";

const StoreContext = createContext<KerfStore | null>(null);

export function KerfProvider({ store, children }: { store: KerfStore; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <StoreContext.Provider value={store}>{children}</StoreContext.Provider>;
}

/** The store itself, for event handlers that read the latest state. */
export function useKerfStore(): KerfStore {
  const store = useContext(StoreContext);
  if (!store) throw new Error("A Kerf screen was drawn outside <KerfProvider>.");
  return store;
}

/**
 * Reads from the store and redraws when the value changes. The selector must return something the
 * store already holds (or a primitive), never a new object, or the screen would redraw forever.
 */
export function useKerf<T>(selector: (s: KerfState & KerfActions) => T): T {
  return useStore(useKerfStore(), selector);
}
```

`ui/TopBar.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The top bar (design spec, "Layout"): the five steps as one switcher, then Undo and Redo.

import { canRedo, canUndo, currentProject } from "../model/history";
import { STEPS } from "../model/steps";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

export function TopBar() {
  const step = useKerf((s) => s.step);
  const setStep = useKerf((s) => s.setStep);
  const name = useKerf((s) => (s.history ? currentProject(s.history).name : null));
  const undoable = useKerf((s) => s.history !== null && canUndo(s.history));
  const redoable = useKerf((s) => s.history !== null && canRedo(s.history));
  const undo = useKerf((s) => s.undo);
  const redo = useKerf((s) => s.redo);
  return (
    <header className="topbar">
      <span className="brand">Kerf</span>
      {name && <span className="project-name">{name}</span>}
      <nav aria-label="Workflow" className="steps-nav">
        <ol className="steps">
          {STEPS.map((s) => (
            <li key={s}>
              <button type="button" className="step" aria-pressed={s === step} onClick={() => setStep(s)}>
                {s}
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </nav>
      <div className="topbar-actions">
        <button type="button" onClick={undo} disabled={!undoable}>
          Undo
        </button>
        <button type="button" onClick={redo} disabled={!redoable}>
          Redo
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { hintFor } from "../model/steps";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";
```

`ui/Notice.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// A short message about what just happened (Interaction spec, "Tweak": "with a notice ... and full
// undo"), or about something the app could not do (ADR 015, rule 11).

import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

export function Notice() {
  const notice = useKerf((s) => s.notice);
  const undo = useKerf((s) => s.undo);
  const dismiss = useKerf((s) => s.dismissNotice);
  if (!notice) return null;
  return (
    <div role="status" className="notice">
      <span>{notice.text}</span>
      {notice.undo && (
        <button type="button" onClick={undo}>
          Undo this
        </button>
      )}
      <button type="button" onClick={dismiss}>
        Dismiss
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`ui/useShortcuts.ts`:

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Keyboard shortcuts for the whole window. Typing in a field is left alone, so Ctrl+Z there undoes
// the typing, not the project.

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

/** True when the key press belongs to a text field or a list, not to the app. */
export function isTyping(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
  return target instanceof HTMLElement && (target.isContentEditable || ["INPUT", "SELECT", "TEXTAREA"].includes(target.tagName));
}

export function useShortcuts(): void {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (isTyping(e.target)) return;
      const s = store.getState();
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "z") {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (e.shiftKey) s.redo();
        else s.undo();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [store]);
}
```

Also make the notice clear on undo: in `store.ts` `undo` already sets `notice: null` (Task 12).

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The Kerf window (Interaction spec, "Screen layout"; design spec, "Layout"). The model view is
// passed in, so the window can be drawn without WebGL in tests.

import { type ReactNode, useEffect } from "react";
import type { KerfStore } from "./model/store";
import { HintLine } from "./ui/HintLine";
import { Notice } from "./ui/Notice";
import { TopBar } from "./ui/TopBar";
import { KerfProvider, useKerf } from "./ui/useKerf";
import { useShortcuts } from "./ui/useShortcuts";

export function App({ store, model }: { store: KerfStore; model: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <KerfProvider store={store}>
      <Window model={model} />
    </KerfProvider>
  );
}

function Window({ model }: { model: ReactNode }) {
  useShortcuts();
  const theme = useKerf((s) => s.theme);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.dataset.theme = theme;
  }, [theme]);
  return (
    <div className="window">
      <TopBar />
      <HintLine />
      <div className="workspace">
        <aside aria-label="Project" className="panel panel-left" />
        <main className="center">
          <section aria-label="Model" className="model">
            {model}
          </section>
        </main>
        <aside aria-label="Inspector" className="panel panel-right" />
      </div>
      <Notice />
    </div>
  );
}
```

`ui/NumberField.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Interaction spec, "Scrubby fields": "drag a field's label left or right to change a dimension …
// click to type an exact value". Steps: ADR 015, rule 2 (1/16" per step, Shift 1", Alt 1/64").

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { NumberField } from "./NumberField";

function field(overrides: Partial<Parameters<typeof NumberField>[0]> = {}) {
  const props = { label: "Width", display: '36"', expr: "36in", onSubmit: vi.fn(() => null), ...overrides };
  render(<NumberField {...props} />);
  return { props, input: screen.getByLabelText("Width") as HTMLInputElement };
}

const drag = (label: HTMLElement, dx: number, keys: { shiftKey?: boolean; altKey?: boolean } = {}) => {
  fireEvent.pointerDown(label, { clientX: 100, pointerId: 1, button: 0 });
  fireEvent.pointerMove(label, { clientX: 100 + dx, pointerId: 1, ...keys });
  fireEvent.pointerUp(label, { clientX: 100 + dx, pointerId: 1 });
};

describe("<NumberField>", () => {
  it("shows the snapped value until the user types", () => {
    expect(field().input.value).toBe('36"');
  });

  it("submits typed text on Enter", () => {
    const { props, input } = field();
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: '30"' } });
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: "Enter" });
    expect(props.onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledWith('30"');
  });

  it("submits typed text when the field loses focus", () => {
    const { props, input } = field();
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: '30"' } });
    fireEvent.blur(input);
    expect(props.onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledWith('30"');
  });

  it("does not submit when nothing was changed", () => {
    const { props, input } = field();
    fireEvent.blur(input);
    expect(props.onSubmit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("keeps refused text, marks the field, and shows the reason under it", () => {
    const { input } = field({ onSubmit: vi.fn(() => '"30" is not a length; add a unit (in, " or mm)') });
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "30" } });
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: "Enter" });
    expect(input.value).toBe("30");
    expect(input.getAttribute("aria-invalid")).toBe("true");
    expect(screen.getByText('"30" is not a length; add a unit (in, " or mm)')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("puts the shown value back on Escape", () => {
    const { input } = field();
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "abc" } });
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: "Escape" });
    expect(input.value).toBe('36"');
  });

  it("changes by 1/16\" for every 4 pixels dragged", () => {
    const onScrub = vi.fn();
    field({ scrub: { value: 7_315_200, units: "imperial", onScrub, onScrubEnd: vi.fn() } });
    drag(screen.getByText("Width"), 40);
    // 40 px = 10 steps × 1/16" (12700) = 127000 → 36" + 5/8".
    expect(onScrub).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(7_442_200);
  });

  it("changes by 1\" per step with Shift and by 1/64\" with Alt", () => {
    const onScrub = vi.fn();
    field({ scrub: { value: 7_315_200, units: "imperial", onScrub, onScrubEnd: vi.fn() } });
    drag(screen.getByText("Width"), 8, { shiftKey: true });
    expect(onScrub).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(7_315_200 + 2 * 203_200);
    drag(screen.getByText("Width"), 8, { altKey: true });
    expect(onScrub).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(7_315_200 + 2 * 3_175);
  });

  it("ends the drag once when the pointer is released", () => {
    const onScrubEnd = vi.fn();
    field({ scrub: { value: 7_315_200, units: "imperial", onScrub: vi.fn(), onScrubEnd } });
    drag(screen.getByText("Width"), 40);
    expect(onScrubEnd).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`ui/SizeFields.test.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Review Focus 5 and Operation specs, resize checks (30" → shelves 29-1/16" cut).

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startProject } from "../model/commands";
import { createStorage } from "../model/storage";
import { createKerfStore, shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { memoryStorage } from "../testing/memory-storage";
import { SizeFields } from "./SizeFields";
import { KerfProvider } from "./useKerf";

function setup() {
  const store = createKerfStore({ storage: createStorage(memoryStorage()) });
  store.getState().apply(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial"));
  const id = shownProject(store.getState())?.structures[0]?.id ?? "";
  render(<KerfProvider store={store}><SizeFields structure={id} /></KerfProvider>);
  return store;
}

const shelfCut = (s: ReturnType<typeof setup>) => s.getState().solved?.result.cutList.lines.find((l) => l.parts.some((p) => p.name === "Shelf"))?.length;

describe("<SizeFields>", () => {
  it("labels a case Width, Height, Depth with its sizes", () => {
    setup();
    expect((screen.getByLabelText("Width") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe('36"');
    expect((screen.getByLabelText("Height") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe('72"');
    expect((screen.getByLabelText("Depth") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe('12"');
  });

  it("resizes to 30\" when typed: shelves 29-1/16\" cut", () => {
    const store = setup();
    const width = screen.getByLabelText("Width");
    fireEvent.change(width, { target: { value: '30"' } });
    fireEvent.blur(width);
    expect(shelfCut(store)).toBe(5_905_500);
  });

  it("records one History entry for a 200-step drag, ending on the exact value", () => {
    const store = setup();
    const label = screen.getByText("Width");
    fireEvent.pointerDown(label, { clientX: 0, pointerId: 1, button: 0 });
    for (let i = 1; i <= 200; i += 1) fireEvent.pointerMove(label, { clientX: -4 * i, pointerId: 1, altKey: true });
    fireEvent.pointerUp(label, { clientX: -800, pointerId: 1 });
    // 200 Alt steps of 1/64" = 3-1/8" narrower: 32-7/8". Shelf cut = 32-7/8 − 2 × 23/32 + 1/2 = 31-15/16" = 6489700.
    expect(store.getState().history?.entries.map((e) => e.label)).toEqual(["New bookcase", 'Width 36" → 32-7/8"']);
    expect(shelfCut(store)).toBe(6_489_700);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add `literalFor` to `size.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * A length as the expression a field stores: an inch fraction for imperial (exact on the 1/64"
 * grid, and the form a woodworker reads) or an exact millimetre literal for metric.
 */
export function literalFor(len: Len, units: Project["units"]): Expr {
  return units === "imperial" ? formatLength(len, "imperial") : exactLiteral(len);
}
```

(import `formatLength` from `@kerf/core`.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `ui/NumberField.tsx`**

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// A scrubby number field (Interaction spec, "Scrubby fields"): drag the label to change the value,
// click the box to type. Steps: ADR 015, rule 2. A refused value stays in the box with the reason
// under it (CODE_QUALITY rule 9), and Escape puts the shown value back.

import type { Len, Project } from "@kerf/core";
import { type PointerEvent, useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from "react";

/** Pixels of drag per step. */
const PIXELS_PER_STEP = 4;

/** Units per drag step: plain, with Shift, with Alt (the grid). */
export const SCRUB_STEPS: Record<Project["units"], { step: Len; shift: Len; alt: Len }> = {
  imperial: { step: 12_700, shift: 203_200, alt: 3_175 },
  metric: { step: 8_000, shift: 80_000, alt: 80 },
};

export interface Scrub {
  value: Len;
  units: Project["units"];
  onScrub(value: Len): void;
  onScrubEnd(): void;
}

interface Props {
  label: string;
  /** The value as shown when not editing ("36\""). */
  display: string;
  /** What the field holds (an expression); typing replaces it. */
  expr: string;
  /** Takes typed text; returns the reason it was refused, or null. */
  onSubmit(text: string): string | null;
  scrub?: Scrub;
}

export function NumberField({ label, display, onSubmit, scrub }: Props) {
  const id = useId();
  const [text, setText] = useState(display);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const drag = useRef<{ x: number; start: Len; moved: boolean } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    setText(display);
    setProblem(null);
  }, [display]);

  const submit = (): void => {
    if (text === display) return;
    setProblem(onSubmit(text));
  };

  const onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLLabelElement>): void => {
    if (!scrub || e.button !== 0) return;
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
    drag.current = { x: e.clientX, start: scrub.value, moved: false };
  };
  const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent<HTMLLabelElement>): void => {
    const d = drag.current;
    if (!scrub || !d) return;
    const steps = Math.trunc((e.clientX - d.x) / PIXELS_PER_STEP);
    if (steps === 0 && !d.moved) return;
    d.moved = true;
    const size = SCRUB_STEPS[scrub.units];
    const unit = e.altKey ? size.alt : e.shiftKey ? size.shift : size.step;
    scrub.onScrub(d.start + steps * unit);
  };
  const onPointerUp = (): void => {
    const d = drag.current;
    drag.current = null;
    if (scrub && d?.moved) scrub.onScrubEnd();
  };

  const messageId = `${id}-problem`;
  return (
    <div className="number-field">
      <label
        htmlFor={id}
        className={scrub ? "scrub-label" : undefined}
        onPointerDown={onPointerDown}
        onPointerMove={onPointerMove}
        onPointerUp={onPointerUp}
      >
        {label}
      </label>
      <input
        id={id}
        value={text}
        aria-invalid={problem !== null}
        aria-describedby={problem ? messageId : undefined}
        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={submit}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === "Enter") submit();
          if (e.key === "Escape") {
            setText(display);
            setProblem(null);
          }
        }}
      />
      {problem && (
        <p id={messageId} className="field-problem">
          {problem}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Note: a drag that never moves is a click, which focuses the box through the label (`htmlFor`). Holding Alt changes the step for the whole drag position, so the tests above move once per drag.

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `ui/SizeFields.tsx`**

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// A structure's overall size (Interaction spec, "Overall size first"), shown in Start and in Shape.
// Typing records one change; dragging previews every step and records one change on release.

import { formatLength, type Id, type Len } from "@kerf/core";
import { useRef } from "react";
import { type Edit, setSize } from "../model/commands";
import { type SizeKey, sizeLabel, sizeOrder } from "../model/labels";
import { literalFor, valueOf } from "../model/size";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { NumberField } from "./NumberField";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

export function SizeFields({ structure }: { structure: Id }) {
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const s = project?.structures.find((x) => x.id === structure);
  if (!project || !s) return null;
  return (
    <div className="fields">
      {sizeOrder(s.type).map((key) => (
        <SizeField key={key} structure={structure} sizeKey={key} />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function SizeField({ structure, sizeKey }: { structure: Id; sizeKey: SizeKey }) {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const project = useKerf((s) => s.history && shownProject(s));
  const lastDrag = useRef<Edit | null>(null);
  const s = project?.structures.find((x) => x.id === structure);
  if (!project || !s) return null;
  const expr = s.size[sizeKey];
  const value = valueOf(expr, project);
  const recorded = () => {
    const h = store.getState().history;
    return h?.entries[h.at]?.project ?? project;
  };
  return (
    <NumberField
      label={sizeLabel(s.type, sizeKey)}
      expr={expr}
      display={value === undefined ? expr : formatLength(value, project.units)}
      onSubmit={(text) => {
        const r = setSize(recorded(), structure, sizeKey, text);
        if (!r.ok) return r.message;
        store.getState().apply(r.edit);
        return null;
      }}
      scrub={
        value === undefined
          ? undefined
          : {
              value,
              units: project.units,
              onScrub: (next: Len) => {
                const r = setSize(recorded(), structure, sizeKey, literalFor(next, project.units));
                if (!r.ok) return;
                lastDrag.current = r.edit;
                store.getState().preview(r.edit.project);
              },
              onScrubEnd: () => {
                if (lastDrag.current) store.getState().commit(lastDrag.current.label);
                lastDrag.current = null;
              },
            }
      }
    />
  );
}
```

(`recorded()` reads History's current entry, so every drag step is computed from the value before the drag and the label reads `Width 36" → 32-7/8"`. A drag step the engine refuses, such as a width of zero, is not previewed; the last good preview stays.)

Styles (append to `styles.css`):

```css
.fields { display: grid; gap: var(--space-2); }
.number-field { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 104px minmax(0, 1fr); gap: var(--space-1) var(--space-2); align-items: center; }
.number-field label, .field label { color: var(--color-text-muted); }
.scrub-label { cursor: ew-resize; user-select: none; touch-action: none; }
.number-field input, .field input, .field select { min-width: 0; padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-2); background: var(--color-bg); color: var(--color-text); border: var(--border-width) solid var(--color-border); border-radius: var(--radius-1); font: inherit; font-variant-numeric: var(--font-numeric); }
.number-field input { text-align: right; }
.number-field input[aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: var(--color-error); }
.field-problem { grid-column: 1 / -1; margin: 0; color: var(--color-error); font-size: var(--font-size-small); }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Store sections**

In `store.ts`, add to `KerfState` `sections: Record<string, boolean>;` and to `KerfActions` `toggleSection(title: string, defaultOpen?: boolean): void;`. Initial value:

```ts
      sections: readSections(deps.storage),
```

with, above `createKerfStore`:

```ts
/** Which sections the user closed or opened (Interaction spec: "Sections remember their open state"). */
function readSections(storage: AppStorage): Record<string, boolean> {
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(storage.read("kerf.sections") ?? "{}");
    if (typeof parsed !== "object" || parsed === null) return {};
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(parsed).filter(([, v]) => typeof v === "boolean")) as Record<string, boolean>;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}
```

and the action:

```ts
      toggleSection: (title, defaultOpen = true) => {
        const sections = { ...get().sections, [title]: !(get().sections[title] ?? defaultOpen) };
        deps.storage.write("kerf.sections", JSON.stringify(sections));
        set({ sections });
      },
```

A damaged `kerf.sections` value only loses which sections were open, so it is read as "none stored" without a notice; put that reason in `readSections`'s doc comment.

`Section.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// A collapsible inspector section (Interaction spec, "Accordion panels"). A closed section draws
// nothing inside it, so hidden fields can never be found or typed into by mistake.

import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

export function Section({ title, defaultOpen = true, children }: { title: string; defaultOpen?: boolean; children: ReactNode }) {
  const open = useKerf((s) => s.sections[title] ?? defaultOpen);
  const toggle = useKerf((s) => s.toggleSection);
  return (
    <section className="section">
      <h2 className="section-title">
        <button type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => toggle(title, defaultOpen)}>
          {title}
        </button>
      </h2>
      {open && <div className="section-body">{children}</div>}
    </section>
  );
}
```

`Inspector.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool

// The right panel (Interaction spec, "Inspector"): what the current step offers.

import { StartStep } from "./StartStep";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

export function Inspector() {
  const step = useKerf((s) => s.step);
  switch (step) {
    case "Start":
      return <StartStep />;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}
```

(Tasks 17-20 replace `default` with their steps; when every step is handled the `default` branch is deleted so TypeScript checks the switch is complete.)

`StartStep.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// 1. Start (Interaction spec): New shows the template cards; a template starts the project; the
// overall size comes first. The shop card sits beside it and never blocks (ADR 015, D4).

import { useState } from "react";
import { startProject, type TemplateName } from "../model/commands";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Section } from "./Section";
import { ShopCard } from "./ShopCard";
import { SizeFields } from "./SizeFields";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

const TEMPLATE_NAMES: TemplateName[] = ["Bookcase", "Keepsake box", "Blank case", "Blank box"];

export function StartStep() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const [choosing, setChoosing] = useState(false);
  const structure = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s)?.structures[0]?.id);
  const start = (name: TemplateName): void => {
    const s = store.getState();
    const edit = startProject(name, s.unitsForNew());
    s.apply(edit);
    s.select({ kind: "structure", structure: edit.project.structures[0]?.id ?? "" });
    setChoosing(false);
  };
  return (
    <div className="step-panel">
      <div className="templates">
        <button type="button" className="primary" onClick={() => setChoosing(!choosing)} aria-expanded={choosing}>
          New
        </button>
        {choosing && (
          <ul className="template-cards">
            {TEMPLATE_NAMES.map((name) => (
              <li key={name}>
                <button type="button" className="template-card" onClick={() => start(name)}>
                  {name}
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>
      {structure && (
        <Section title="Size">
          <SizeFields structure={structure} />
        </Section>
      )}
      <Section title="Your shop">
        <ShopCard />
      </Section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`ShopCard.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The shop checklist (Interaction spec, "4. Joinery from your shop": table saw kerf, dado stack
// max width, router with straight bits and roundover bits, hand tools for dovetails). Every change
// is checked by the engine (`shopProblem`) before it is kept.

import { formatLength, type Len, parseLength, type ShopProfile, shopProblem } from "@kerf/core";
import { NumberField } from "./NumberField";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

type Tool = ShopProfile["tools"][number];
const IN = 203_200;

/** "1/8\", 1/4\"" → lengths, or the first entry that is not a length. */
function readBits(text: string): Len[] | string {
  const parts = text.split(",").map((t) => t.trim()).filter((t) => t !== "");
  const out: Len[] = [];
  for (const part of parts) {
    try {
      out.push(parseLength(part, "imperial"));
    } catch {
      return `"${part}" is not a bit size; write sizes like 1/4", 1/2".`;
    }
  }
  return out;
}

const showBits = (bits: Len[]): string => bits.map((b) => formatLength(b, "imperial")).join(", ");

export function ShopCard() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const shop = useKerf((s) => s.shop);
  const tool = <T extends Tool["type"]>(type: T) => shop.tools.find((t): t is Extract<Tool, { type: T }> => t.type === type);
  const dado = tool("dadoStack");
  const router = tool("router");
  const hand = tool("handTools");

  /** Keeps the shop when the engine accepts it; otherwise returns the engine's reason. */
  const save = (next: ShopProfile): string | null => {
    const problem = shopProblem(next);
    if (problem) return problem.message;
    store.getState().setShop(next);
    return null;
  };
  const withTool = (type: Tool["type"], replacement: Tool | undefined): ShopProfile => ({
    ...shop,
    tools: [...shop.tools.filter((t) => t.type !== type), ...(replacement ? [replacement] : [])],
  });

  return (
    <div className="shop-card">
      <NumberField
        label="Blade kerf"
        display={formatLength(shop.kerf, "imperial")}
        expr={formatLength(shop.kerf, "imperial")}
        onSubmit={(text) => {
          try {
            return save({ ...shop, kerf: parseLength(text, "imperial") });
          } catch (e) {
            return e instanceof Error ? e.message : "That is not a length.";
          }
        }}
      />
      <label className="check">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={dado !== undefined} onChange={(e) => save(withTool("dadoStack", e.target.checked ? { type: "dadoStack", maxWidth: (3 * IN) / 4 } : undefined))} />
        Dado stack
      </label>
      {dado && (
        <NumberField
          label="Widest dado"
          display={formatLength(dado.maxWidth, "imperial")}
          expr={formatLength(dado.maxWidth, "imperial")}
          onSubmit={(text) => {
            try {
              return save(withTool("dadoStack", { type: "dadoStack", maxWidth: parseLength(text, "imperial") }));
            } catch (e) {
              return e instanceof Error ? e.message : "That is not a length.";
            }
          }}
        />
      )}
      <label className="check">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={router !== undefined} onChange={(e) => save(withTool("router", e.target.checked ? { type: "router", straightBits: [], roundoverBits: [] } : undefined))} />
        Router
      </label>
      {router && (
        <>
          <NumberField
            label="Straight bits"
            display={showBits(router.straightBits)}
            expr={showBits(router.straightBits)}
            onSubmit={(text) => {
              const bits = readBits(text);
              return typeof bits === "string" ? bits : save(withTool("router", { ...router, straightBits: bits }));
            }}
          />
          <NumberField
            label="Roundover bits"
            display={showBits(router.roundoverBits)}
            expr={showBits(router.roundoverBits)}
            onSubmit={(text) => {
              const bits = readBits(text);
              return typeof bits === "string" ? bits : save(withTool("router", { ...router, roundoverBits: bits }));
            }}
          />
        </>
      )}
      <label className="check">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={hand?.dovetails === true} onChange={(e) => save(withTool("handTools", e.target.checked ? { type: "handTools", dovetails: true } : undefined))} />
        Dovetails by hand
      </label>
    </div>
  );
}
```

The shop's lengths are shown in inches whatever the project's units, because the shop is per person, not per project. Add to ADR 015 rule 11 in this task's commit: "The shop card shows inches; a metric shop display is a later setting."

App wiring: in `App.tsx` render `<Inspector />` inside the right `aside`.

```css
.section { border-bottom: var(--border-width) solid var(--color-border); }
.section-title { margin: 0; font-size: var(--font-size-panel); }
.section-title button { width: 100%; text-align: left; padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border: 0; background: none; font-weight: var(--font-weight-strong); }
.section-title button::after { content: "▾"; float: right; color: var(--color-text-muted); }
.section-title button[aria-expanded="false"]::after { content: "▸"; }
.section-body { display: grid; gap: var(--space-2); padding: 0 var(--space-3) var(--space-3); }
.template-cards { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: var(--space-2); margin: var(--space-2) 0 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; }
.template-card { width: 100%; min-height: 56px; }
.primary { background: var(--color-accent); color: var(--color-step-active-text); border-color: var(--color-accent); font-weight: var(--font-weight-strong); }
.check { display: flex; gap: var(--space-2); align-items: center; }
```

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Interaction spec, "2. Shape" table (Case: carcase stock; top/bottom between sides or sides
// between; back none / inset in rabbets / applied; back stock). Labels: end-to-end contract.

import { solve } from "@kerf/core";
import { loadShopProfile } from "@kerf/fixtures";
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startProject } from "../model/commands";
import { createStorage } from "../model/storage";
import { createKerfStore, shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { memoryStorage } from "../testing/memory-storage";
import { Inspector } from "./Inspector";
import { KerfProvider } from "./useKerf";

function setup(template: "Bookcase" | "Keepsake box" = "Bookcase") {
  const store = createKerfStore({ storage: createStorage(memoryStorage()) });
  store.getState().apply(startProject(template, "imperial"));
  store.getState().setStep("Shape");
  render(<KerfProvider store={store}><Inspector /></KerfProvider>);
  return store;
}

const select = (label: string) => screen.getByLabelText(label, { exact: true }) as HTMLSelectElement;
const optionText = (s: HTMLSelectElement) => s.options[s.selectedIndex]?.textContent;

describe("the Shape step", () => {
  it("shows the bookcase's form as the template set it", () => {
    setup();
    expect(optionText(select("Carcase stock"))).toBe("3/4 plywood");
    expect(optionText(select("Top and bottom"))).toBe("Between the sides");
    expect(optionText(select("Back"))).toBe("Inset in rabbets");
    expect(optionText(select("Back stock"))).toBe("1/4 plywood");
  });

  it("removes the back: the back stock field goes and the cut list loses its back line", () => {
    const store = setup();
    fireEvent.change(select("Back"), { target: { value: "none" } });
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("Back stock")).toBeNull();
    expect(store.getState().history?.entries.at(-1)?.label).toBe("Back: none");
    const p = shownProject(store.getState());
    if (!p) throw new Error("no project");
    // Operation specs: the back is its own line; without it 2 lines remain.
    expect(solve(p, loadShopProfile()).cutList.lines).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("keeps advanced depths behind More, closed at first", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("Dado depth")).toBeNull();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "More" }));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Dado depth")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("shows a box's walls, corners and bottom", () => {
    setup("Keepsake box");
    expect(optionText(select("Wall stock"))).toBe("4/4 walnut");
    expect(optionText(select("Corners"))).toBe("Through dovetails");
    expect(optionText(select("Bottom"))).toBe("In a groove");
    expect((screen.getByLabelText("Grain wraps the corners") as HTMLInputElement).checked).toBe(true);
  });

  it("has no button named Joinery, so the step switcher stays unique", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.queryByRole("button", { name: /joinery/i })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`Choice.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// A labelled list of choices (a form field with a fixed set of answers).

import { useId } from "react";

interface Props {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  options: ReadonlyArray<{ value: string; label: string }>;
  onChange(value: string): void;
}

export function Choice({ label, value, options, onChange }: Props) {
  const id = useId();
  return (
    <div className="field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <select id={id} value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}>
        {options.map((o) => (
          <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>
            {o.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`ShapeStep.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// 2. Shape (Interaction spec): the structure form. Advanced settings sit behind More
// ("Fewer panels, progressive disclosure"). Each change is one named History entry.

import type { Id, Project, Structure } from "@kerf/core";
import { setOption } from "../model/commands";
import { CHOICES, optionLabel } from "../model/labels";
import { readLength } from "../model/size";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Choice } from "./Choice";
import { NumberField } from "./NumberField";
import { Section } from "./Section";
import { SizeFields } from "./SizeFields";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

export function ShapeStep() {
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const structure = project?.structures[0];
  if (!project || !structure) return <p className="empty">Start a project first: go to Start and click New.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="step-panel">
      <Section title="Size">
        <SizeFields structure={structure.id} />
      </Section>
      {structure.type === "case" ? <CaseForm project={project} structure={structure} /> : <BoxForm project={project} structure={structure} />}
      <Section title="More" defaultOpen={false}>
        <Depths project={project} structure={structure} />
      </Section>
    </div>
  );
}

interface FormProps {
  project: Project;
  structure: Structure;
}

/** Sets one form option as a named change. */
function useOption(structure: Id) {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  return (key: string, value: string | boolean): void => {
    const p = shownProject(store.getState());
    if (p) store.getState().apply(setOption(p, structure, key, value));
  };
}

const text = (s: Structure, key: string, fallback: string): string => {
  const v = s.options[key];
  return typeof v === "string" ? v : fallback;
};

function StockChoice({ project, structure, field }: FormProps & { field: string }) {
  const set = useOption(structure.id);
  return (
    <Choice
      label={optionLabel(field)}
      value={text(structure, field, "")}
      options={project.stock.map((s) => ({ value: s.id, label: s.name }))}
      onChange={(v) => set(field, v)}
    />
  );
}

function CaseForm({ project, structure }: FormProps) {
  const set = useOption(structure.id);
  const back = text(structure, "back", "none");
  return (
    <>
      <Section title="Construction">
        <StockChoice project={project} structure={structure} field="carcaseStock" />
        <Choice label="Top and bottom" value={text(structure, "topBottom", "between-sides")} options={CHOICES.topBottom} onChange={(v) => set("topBottom", v)} />
      </Section>
      <Section title="Back">
        <Choice label="Back" value={back} options={CHOICES.back} onChange={(v) => set("back", v)} />
        {back !== "none" && <StockChoice project={project} structure={structure} field="backStock" />}
      </Section>
    </>
  );
}

function BoxForm({ project, structure }: FormProps) {
  const set = useOption(structure.id);
  return (
    <>
      <Section title="Walls">
        <StockChoice project={project} structure={structure} field="wallStock" />
        <Choice label="Corners" value={text(structure, "cornerJoint", "through-dovetail")} options={CHOICES.cornerJoint} onChange={(v) => set("cornerJoint", v)} />
        <label className="check">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={structure.options.grainWraps === true} onChange={(e) => set("grainWraps", e.target.checked)} />
          Grain wraps the corners
        </label>
      </Section>
      <Section title="Bottom panel">
        <Choice label="Bottom" value={text(structure, "bottom", "groove")} options={CHOICES.bottom} onChange={(v) => set("bottom", v)} />
        {text(structure, "bottom", "groove") !== "none" && <StockChoice project={project} structure={structure} field="bottomStock" />}
      </Section>
    </>
  );
}

const CASE_DEPTHS = ["dadoDepth", "rabbetDepth", "backRabbetDepth"] as const;
const BOX_DEPTHS = ["rabbetDepth", "grooveDepth", "grooveOffset"] as const;

/** Joint depths; blank means the spec's default rule (ADR 014, D1), which the engine reads as absent. */
function Depths({ project, structure }: FormProps) {
  const set = useOption(structure.id);
  const keys = structure.type === "case" ? CASE_DEPTHS : BOX_DEPTHS;
  return (
    <>
      {keys.map((key) => {
        const value = text(structure, key, "");
        return (
          <NumberField
            key={key}
            label={optionLabel(key)}
            display={value === "" ? "Default" : value}
            expr={value}
            onSubmit={(typed) => {
              const t = typed.trim();
              if (t === "" || t === "Default") {
                set(key, "");
                return null;
              }
              const read = readLength(t, project);
              if (!read.ok) return read.message;
              set(key, read.expr);
              return null;
            }}
          />
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `model/tree.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The project tree (Interaction spec, "Project tree (left)"): structures, their openings, then the
// boards with counts, each with how many problems the engine reported on it.

import type { Id, Opening, PlacedPart, Project, SolveResult, Structure } from "@kerf/core";
import { readCount } from "./size";

export type TreeRow =
  | { kind: "structure"; key: string; depth: 0; label: string; structure: Id; problems: number }
  | { kind: "opening"; key: string; depth: number; label: string; structure: Id; opening: Id; problems: number }
  | { kind: "group"; key: string; depth: number; label: string; count: number; instances: Id[]; testId: string; problems: number };

/** Plural of a role for test ids (`part-group-shelves`): the end-to-end contract. */
const PLURAL: Record<string, string> = { shelf: "shelves", side: "sides", top: "tops", bottom: "bottoms", back: "backs", partition: "partitions", front: "fronts" };

const slug = (s: string): string => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "");

/** "Opening · 4 shelves": a count that names a parameter is shown as its value. */
function describeOpening(o: Opening, project: Project): string {
  const d = o.division;
  if (!d) return "Opening · empty";
  if (d.type === "partition") return `Opening · partition at ${d.at}`;
  const n = readCount(d.count, project);
  const count = n.ok ? String(n.value) : d.count;
  return d.type === "shelves" ? `Opening · ${count} shelves` : `Opening · ${count} bays`;
}

/** The first structure is called by the project's name; later ones as the engine numbers them. */
function structureLabel(project: Project, s: Structure, index: number): string {
  if (index === 0) return project.name;
  return `${s.type === "case" ? "Case" : "Box"} ${index + 1}`;
}

/** The name a group of boards shows: "Side" for Right side and Left side. */
function groupLabel(p: PlacedPart): string {
  if (p.source && !p.detached) return p.source.role.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + p.source.role.slice(1);
  return p.name;
}

export function treeRows(project: Project, result: SolveResult): TreeRow[] {
  const problemsOn = (parts: Set<Id>): number =>
    result.diagnostics.filter((d) => d.severity !== "info" && d.part !== undefined && parts.has(d.part)).length;
  const rows: TreeRow[] = [];

  const openingRows = (s: Structure, list: Opening[], depth: number): void => {
    for (const o of list) {
      rows.push({ kind: "opening", key: `opening:${o.id}`, depth, label: describeOpening(o, project), structure: s.id, opening: o.id, problems: 0 });
      openingRows(s, o.children, depth + 1);
    }
  };

  const groups = new Map<string, { label: string; testId: string; placed: PlacedPart[] }>();
  for (const p of result.placements) {
    const key = p.source && !p.detached ? `${p.source.structure}:${p.source.role}` : `part:${p.part}`;
    const testId = `part-group-${p.source && !p.detached ? (PLURAL[p.source.role] ?? slug(p.source.role)) : slug(p.name)}`;
    const group = groups.get(key) ?? { label: groupLabel(p), testId, placed: [] };
    group.placed.push(p);
    groups.set(key, group);
  }

  for (const [index, s] of project.structures.entries()) {
    rows.push({ kind: "structure", key: `structure:${s.id}`, depth: 0, label: structureLabel(project, s, index), structure: s.id, problems: 0 });
    openingRows(s, s.openings, 1);
  }
  for (const [key, g] of groups) {
    rows.push({
      kind: "group",
      key,
      depth: 1,
      label: g.label,
      count: g.placed.length,
      instances: g.placed.map((p) => p.instance),
      testId: g.testId,
      problems: problemsOn(new Set(g.placed.map((p) => p.part))),
    });
  }
  return rows;
}
```



- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `ui/ProjectTree.tsx` and `ui/HistoryPanel.tsx`**

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The project tree (Interaction spec, "Project tree (left)"). Rows are tree items, not buttons
// (design spec, end-to-end contract), and keyboard users move with the arrow keys and Enter.

import type { KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { type TreeRow, treeRows } from "../model/tree";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

export function ProjectTree() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const result = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result);
  const selection = useKerf((s) => s.selection);
  if (!project || !result) return <p className="empty">No project yet. Click New in Start.</p>;
  const rows = treeRows(project, result);

  const pick = (row: TreeRow): void => {
    const s = store.getState();
    if (row.kind === "structure") s.select({ kind: "structure", structure: row.structure });
    if (row.kind === "opening") s.select({ kind: "opening", structure: row.structure, opening: row.opening });
    if (row.kind === "group") s.select({ kind: "boards", instances: row.instances });
  };
  const isSelected = (row: TreeRow): boolean =>
    (row.kind === "structure" && selection.kind === "structure" && selection.structure === row.structure) ||
    (row.kind === "opening" && selection.kind === "opening" && selection.opening === row.opening) ||
    (row.kind === "group" && selection.kind === "boards" && row.instances.every((i) => selection.instances.includes(i)));
  const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLLIElement>, row: TreeRow): void => {
    if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") pick(row);
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") (e.currentTarget.nextElementSibling as HTMLElement | null)?.focus();
    if (e.key === "ArrowUp") (e.currentTarget.previousElementSibling as HTMLElement | null)?.focus();
  };

  return (
    <ul role="tree" aria-label="Boards and openings" className="tree">
      {rows.map((row) => (
        <li
          key={row.key}
          role="treeitem"
          tabIndex={0}
          aria-selected={isSelected(row)}
          aria-level={row.depth + 1}
          data-testid={row.kind === "opening" ? "opening" : row.kind === "group" ? row.testId : undefined}
          className="tree-row"
          style={{ paddingLeft: `calc(var(--space-3) + ${row.depth} * var(--space-4))` }}
          onClick={() => pick(row)}
          onKeyDown={(e) => onKey(e, row)}
        >
          <span className="tree-label">{row.label}</span>
          {row.kind === "group" && <span className="tree-count">{row.count}</span>}
          {row.problems > 0 && <span className="badge">{row.problems}</span>}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

(The panel is the region named "Project"; the tree inside it is named "Boards and openings", so each name is used once.)

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The History list (Interaction spec, "History panel"): every change in shop words; click an entry
// to step back to it. Entries are list options, not buttons, so no entry's words ("New bookcase")
// can be mistaken for a command.

import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

export function HistoryPanel() {
  const history = useKerf((s) => s.history);
  const jumpTo = useKerf((s) => s.jumpTo);
  if (!history) return null;
  return (
    <div className="history">
      <h2 className="panel-heading">History</h2>
      <ol role="listbox" aria-label="History" className="history-list">
        {history.entries.map((entry, index) => (
          <li
            // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: entries have no id; position is their identity in History.
            key={index}
            role="option"
            tabIndex={0}
            aria-selected={index === history.at}
            className={index > history.at ? "history-entry undone" : "history-entry"}
            onClick={() => jumpTo(index)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") jumpTo(index);
            }}
          >
            {entry.label}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `ui/DivideStep.tsx`**

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// 3. Divide (Interaction spec): for the picked opening, add shelves (count, evenly spaced or at set
// heights), a partition (a length or a fraction from the left), or N bays.

import type { Id, Opening, Project } from "@kerf/core";
import { useState } from "react";
import { addPartition, clearDivision, type EditResult, setShelves, splitBays } from "../model/commands";
import { readCount, readLength } from "../model/size";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Choice } from "./Choice";
import { NumberField } from "./NumberField";
import { Section } from "./Section";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

type Mode = "shelves" | "partition" | "bays" | null;

function findOpening(list: Opening[], id: Id): Opening | undefined {
  for (const o of list) {
    if (o.id === id) return o;
    const inner = findOpening(o.children, id);
    if (inner) return inner;
  }
  return undefined;
}

export function DivideStep() {
  const selection = useKerf((s) => s.selection);
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  if (selection.kind !== "opening" || !project) return <p className="empty">Click an opening in the project tree.</p>;
  const structure = project.structures.find((s) => s.id === selection.structure);
  const opening = structure && findOpening(structure.openings, selection.opening);
  if (!structure || !opening) return <p className="empty">Click an opening in the project tree.</p>;
  return <OpeningForm key={opening.id} project={project} structure={structure.id} opening={opening} />;
}

function OpeningForm({ project, structure, opening }: { project: Project; structure: Id; opening: Opening }) {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>(opening.division?.type ?? null);
  const run = (result: EditResult): string | null => {
    if (!result.ok) return result.message;
    store.getState().apply(result.edit);
    return null;
  };
  const shelves = opening.division?.type === "shelves" ? opening.division : undefined;
  const count = shelves ? readCount(shelves.count, project) : undefined;
  const countText = count?.ok ? String(count.value) : "";
  const spacing = shelves && shelves.spacing !== "even" ? "heights" : "even";

  return (
    <div className="step-panel">
      <Section title="Opening">
        <div className="button-row">
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={mode === "shelves"} onClick={() => setMode("shelves")}>Add shelves</button>
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={mode === "partition"} onClick={() => setMode("partition")}>Add partition</button>
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={mode === "bays"} onClick={() => setMode("bays")}>Split into bays</button>
          {opening.division && (
            <button type="button" onClick={() => store.getState().apply(clearDivision(project, structure, opening.id))}>Empty the opening</button>
          )}
        </div>
        {mode === "shelves" && (
          <>
            <NumberField label="Count" display={countText} expr={countText} onSubmit={(t) => run(setShelves(project, structure, opening.id, t, shelves?.spacing ?? "even"))} />
            <Choice
              label="Spacing"
              value={spacing}
              options={[{ value: "even", label: "Evenly spaced" }, { value: "heights", label: "At set heights" }]}
              onChange={(v) => {
                if (v === "even") run(setShelves(project, structure, opening.id, countText || "1", "even"));
                else run(setShelves(project, structure, opening.id, countText || "1", Array.isArray(shelves?.spacing) ? shelves.spacing : []));
              }}
            />
            {spacing === "heights" && (
              <NumberField
                label="Heights from the bottom"
                display={Array.isArray(shelves?.spacing) ? shelves.spacing.join(", ") : ""}
                expr=""
                onSubmit={(t) => {
                  const heights = t.split(",").map((h) => h.trim()).filter((h) => h !== "");
                  for (const h of heights) {
                    const r = readLength(h, project);
                    if (!r.ok) return r.message;
                  }
                  return run(setShelves(project, structure, opening.id, String(heights.length), heights));
                }}
              />
            )}
          </>
        )}
        {mode === "partition" && (
          <NumberField label="From the left" display={opening.division?.type === "partition" ? opening.division.at : ""} expr="" onSubmit={(t) => run(addPartition(project, structure, opening.id, t))} />
        )}
        {mode === "bays" && (
          <NumberField label="Number of bays" display={opening.division?.type === "bays" ? opening.division.count : ""} expr="" onSubmit={(t) => run(splitBays(project, structure, opening.id, t))} />
        )}
      </Section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

(Label check against the Global Constraints: "Count", "Spacing", "Heights from the bottom", "From the left", "Number of bays". "Number of bays" contains no binding label; "Count" must be the only label containing "count", so do not name the bays field "Bay count".)

Spacing and an unchanged count produce an identical project, which Step 3's store rule ignores, so the end-to-end test's "fill 4, choose Evenly spaced" records nothing new.

In `Inspector.tsx` add `case "Divide": return <DivideStep />;`.

```ts
describe("joineryRows()", () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `model/joinery.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The Joinery step's list (Interaction spec, "4. Joinery from your shop"): each joint in shop words,
// its cut sizes, and whether the user's tools can make it, or what they would need.

import { formatLength, type Id, type JointCut, type JointSummary, type Project } from "@kerf/core";

export interface JoineryRow {
  joint: Id;
  /** "Shelf in dados in Right side". */
  text: string;
  /** Cut sizes: "23/32\" wide, 1/4\" deep". */
  detail: string;
  missing: boolean;
  /** What the shop can do about it, as a sentence. */
  shop: string;
}

const KIND = { dado: "dados", groove: "grooves", rabbet: "rabbets", dovetail: "dovetails" } as const;

function shopSentence(cut: JointCut, units: Project["units"]): string {
  if (cut.kind === "dovetail") return cut.missing ? "You'd need hand tools for dovetails." : "Cut by hand.";
  if (cut.missing) return `You'd need a dado stack, or a router with a straight bit no wider than ${formatLength(cut.width, units)}.`;
  return cut.tool === "dadoStack" ? "Your dado stack cuts this." : "Your router cuts this, in more than one pass if the bit is narrower.";
}

export function joineryRows(joints: JointSummary[], units: Project["units"]): JoineryRow[] {
  return joints.flatMap((j) =>
    j.cuts.map((cut) => ({
      joint: j.joint,
      text: `${j.guest} in ${KIND[cut.kind]} in ${j.host}`,
      detail: cut.kind === "dovetail" ? `${j.pairs} corner${j.pairs === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : `${formatLength(cut.width, units)} wide, ${formatLength(cut.depth, units)} deep`,
      missing: cut.missing,
      shop: shopSentence(cut, units),
    })),
  );
}
```

(A joint whose `cuts` is empty because it failed has its reason in the diagnostics; show those in Task 20's problems list, not here.)
