```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// 4. Joinery (Interaction spec; ADR 015, D2): the joints the form chose, what the shop can cut, and
// Accept all. Where the engine offers another joint (box corners) the choice is here too.

import { acceptJoinery, setOption } from "../model/commands";
import { joineryRows } from "../model/joinery";
import { CHOICES } from "../model/labels";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Choice } from "./Choice";
import { Section } from "./Section";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

export function JoineryStep() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const joints = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result.joints);
  if (!project || !joints) return <p className="empty">Start a project first: go to Start and click New.</p>;
  const rows = joineryRows(joints, project.units);
  const reviewed = project.structures.length > 0 && project.structures.every((s) => s.options.joineryReviewed === true);
  const box = project.structures.find((s) => s.type === "box");
  return (
    <div className="step-panel">
      <Section title="Proposed joints">
        <ul className="joint-list">
          {rows.map((row) => (
            <li key={`${row.joint}:${row.text}`} className={row.missing ? "joint missing" : "joint"}>
              <span className="joint-text">{row.text}</span>
              <span className="joint-detail">{row.detail}</span>
              <span className="joint-shop">{row.shop}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
        {box && (
          <Choice
            label="Corners"
            value={typeof box.options.cornerJoint === "string" ? box.options.cornerJoint : "through-dovetail"}
            options={CHOICES.cornerJoint}
            onChange={(v) => store.getState().apply(setOption(project, box.id, "cornerJoint", v))}
          />
        )}
        {reviewed ? (
          <p className="ok">Joinery accepted. You can still change any joint.</p>
        ) : (
          <button type="button" className="primary" onClick={() => store.getState().apply(acceptJoinery(project))}>
            Accept all
          </button>
        )}
      </Section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Commands**

In `commands.ts` (import `attachCopyIn`, `attachPartIn`, `type Instance`):

```ts
/** The instance a placed copy is now: a copy taken out of a row becomes an instance of its own. */
export function instanceOf(project: Project, placed: PlacedPart): Instance | undefined {
  if (placed.pattern) {
    const { id, index } = placed.pattern;
    const own = project.instances.find((i) => i.detachedFrom?.pattern === id && i.detachedFrom.index === index);
    if (own) return own;
  }
  return project.instances.find((i) => i.id === placed.instance);
}

/** Hands a taken-over board back to its form (Domain model: "Undo re-attaches"). */
export function handBack(project: Project, placed: PlacedPart): Edit {
  const own = project.instances.find((i) => i.id === placed.instance);
  const next = own?.detachedFrom ? attachCopyIn(project, own.id) : attachPartIn(project, placed.part);
  return { project: next, label: `${placed.name} follows the form again` };
}
```

and change `addRoundover` to find the part through `instanceOf(taken.project, placed)?.def ?? placed.part`.

- [ ] **Step 4: `ui/Problems.tsx` and `ui/BoardInspector.tsx`**

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The engine's problems in its own words (Interaction spec, "Feedback & errors": "the inspector
// explains it").

import type { Id } from "@kerf/core";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

export function Problems({ parts }: { parts?: Set<Id> }) {
  const diagnostics = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result.diagnostics);
  const shown = (diagnostics ?? []).filter((d) => d.severity !== "info" && (!parts || (d.part !== undefined && parts.has(d.part))));
  if (shown.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <ul className="problems">
      {shown.map((d, i) => (
        // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: diagnostics have no id and do not reorder within one solve.
        <li key={i} className={d.severity === "error" ? "problem error" : "problem warning"}>
          {d.message}
          {d.count !== undefined && d.count > 1 ? ` (${d.count} times)` : ""}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The selected boards (Interaction spec, "Inspector": "a part's properties ('Length 34-9/16" · cut
// 35-1/16"')"), where they came from, and their problems. Shown as a list of facts, not fields.

import { formatLength, type Id, type PlacedPart, type Project, type SolveResult } from "@kerf/core";
import { handBack } from "../model/commands";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Problems } from "./Problems";
import { Section } from "./Section";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

/** "34-9/16\" · cut 35-1/16\"" when the cut differs from the visible size (Domain model, decision 3). */
function sized(visible: number, cut: number | undefined, units: Project["units"]): string {
  const v = formatLength(visible, units);
  return cut === undefined || cut === visible ? v : `${v} · cut ${formatLength(cut, units)}`;
}

function cutLineOf(result: SolveResult, placed: PlacedPart) {
  return result.cutList.lines.find((l) => l.parts.some((p) => p.name === placed.name));
}

export function BoardInspector({ instances }: { instances: Id[] }) {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const result = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result);
  if (!project || !result) return null;
  const placed = result.placements.filter((p) => instances.includes(p.instance));
  const first = placed[0];
  if (!first) return <p className="empty">The selected boards are no longer in the project.</p>;
  const names = new Set(placed.map((p) => p.name));
  const title = names.size === 1 ? `${first.name}${placed.length > 1 ? ` × ${placed.length}` : ""}` : `${placed.length} boards`;
  const line = cutLineOf(result, first);
  const stock = project.stock.find((s) => s.id === project.parts.find((d) => d.id === first.part)?.stock);
  const origin = first.source
    ? first.detached
      ? "Custom: the form won't change it."
      : "Made by the form. Editing one by hand takes it over."
    : "Made by hand.";
  return (
    <div className="step-panel">
      <h2 className="board-title">{title}</h2>
      <Section title="Board">
        <dl className="facts">
          <dt>Length</dt>
          <dd>{sized(line?.visible.length ?? first.size.length, line?.length, project.units)}</dd>
          <dt>Width</dt>
          <dd>{sized(line?.visible.width ?? first.size.width, line?.width, project.units)}</dd>
          <dt>Thickness</dt>
          <dd>{formatLength(first.size.thickness, project.units)}</dd>
          {stock && (
            <>
              <dt>Stock</dt>
              <dd>{stock.name}</dd>
            </>
          )}
        </dl>
        <p className="origin">{origin}</p>
        {first.detached && names.size === 1 && (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => store.getState().apply(handBack(project, first))}>
            Follow the form again
          </button>
        )}
      </Section>
      <Problems parts={new Set(placed.map((p) => p.part))} />
    </div>
  );
}
```

Careful: `<dt>Width</dt>` is text, not a label, so it does not collide with the Width field (labels only). Verify with `screen.getAllByLabelText("Width")` returning only the size field in a combined test if both panels are visible at once (they are not: the board panel replaces the step panel).

In `Inspector.tsx`, before the step switch:


- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `model/plan-text.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The build plan in shop words (Interaction spec, "6. The build plan"). Formatting only: every
// number comes from the engine's solve.

import { type CutListLine, formatLength, type MachiningSetup, type Project, type StockTotals } from "@kerf/core";

const TOOL = { dadoStack: "Dado stack", router: "Router", handTools: "Hand tools" } as const;

export function setupText(setup: MachiningSetup, units: Project["units"]): string {
  const cuts = `${setup.cuts} cut${setup.cuts === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  const lacking = setup.missing ? " (you don't have this tool)" : "";
  if (setup.tool === "handTools") return `Hand-cut dovetails: ${setup.cuts} joint${setup.cuts === 1 ? "" : "s"}${lacking}`;
  if (setup.bit) return `Router, ${formatLength(setup.bit.radius, units)} roundover bit: ${setup.cuts} edge${setup.cuts === 1 ? "" : "s"}${lacking}`;
  return `${TOOL[setup.tool]}, ${formatLength(setup.width, units)} wide, ${formatLength(setup.depth, units)} deep: ${cuts}${lacking}`;
}

export function cutLineText(line: CutListLine, project: Project): { parts: string; stock: string; cut: string; visible: string } {
  const f = (n: number): string => formatLength(n, project.units);
  return {
    parts: line.parts.map((p) => (p.quantity > 1 ? `${p.name} × ${p.quantity}` : p.name)).join(", "),
    stock: project.stock.find((s) => s.id === line.stock)?.name ?? "",
    cut: `${f(line.length)} × ${f(line.width)} × ${f(line.thickness)}`,
    visible: `${f(line.visible.length)} × ${f(line.visible.width)}${line.visibleVaries ? " (varies)" : ""}`,
  };
}

/** A rational shown to one decimal place, using whole-number arithmetic only. */
function tenths(value: { num: bigint; den: bigint }): string {
  const t = (value.num * 10n + value.den / 2n) / value.den;
  return `${t / 10n}.${t % 10n}`;
}

export function totalsText(totals: StockTotals, project: Project): string {
  const name = project.stock.find((s) => s.id === totals.stock)?.name ?? "";
  if (totals.sheets !== undefined) return `${name}: ${totals.sheets} sheet${totals.sheets === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  const parts = [
    totals.boards !== undefined ? `${totals.boards} board${totals.boards === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : "",
    totals.boardFeet ? `${tenths(totals.boardFeet)} board feet` : "",
    totals.linearFeet ? `${tenths(totals.linearFeet)} linear feet` : "",
  ].filter((p) => p !== "");
  return `${name}: ${parts.join(", ")}`;
}
```

`ui/Drawer.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The build-plan drawer (Interaction spec, "Build plan drawer (bottom)"): live on every edit, and a
// banner instead of a false plan while any error exists ("The build plan never lies").

import type { DrawerTab } from "../model/store";
import { AssemblyTab } from "./AssemblyTab";
import { CutListTab } from "./CutListTab";
import { SetupsTab } from "./SetupsTab";
import { SheetsTab } from "./SheetsTab";
import { ShoppingTab } from "./ShoppingTab";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

const TABS: DrawerTab[] = ["Shopping", "Cut list", "Sheets", "Setups", "Assembly"];

export function Drawer() {
  const tab = useKerf((s) => s.drawerTab);
  const setTab = useKerf((s) => s.setDrawerTab);
  const tall = useKerf((s) => s.step === "Build plan");
  const result = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result);
  const firstError = result?.diagnostics.find((d) => d.severity === "error");
  return (
    <section aria-label="Drawer" className={tall ? "drawer tall" : "drawer"}>
      <div role="tablist" className="tabs">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <button key={t} type="button" role="tab" id={`tab-${t}`} aria-selected={t === tab} aria-controls="drawer-panel" onClick={() => setTab(t)}>
            {t}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {firstError && (
        <p role="alert" className="banner">
          Not ready to build: {firstError.message}
        </p>
      )}
      <div role="tabpanel" id="drawer-panel" aria-labelledby={`tab-${tab}`} className="tab-panel">
        {tab === "Shopping" && <ShoppingTab />}
        {tab === "Cut list" && <CutListTab />}
        {tab === "Sheets" && <SheetsTab />}
        {tab === "Setups" && <SetupsTab />}
        {tab === "Assembly" && <AssemblyTab />}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

(Note `aria-labelledby` on the panel names it after a tab, never "Width" etc. The drawer region is labelled "Drawer", not "Build plan", so the step button stays the only "Build plan" button and no label contains it.)

`ui/CutListTab.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The cut list (Cut list & nesting): one line per identical cut size, with the visible size beside
// the cut size (Domain model, decision 3).

import { cutLineText } from "../model/plan-text";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

export function CutListTab() {
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const lines = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result.cutList.lines);
  if (!project || !lines) return null;
  return (
    <table className="plan-table">
      <thead>
        <tr>
          <th scope="col">Parts</th>
          <th scope="col">Qty</th>
          <th scope="col">Stock</th>
          <th scope="col">Cut</th>
          <th scope="col">Visible</th>
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {lines.map((line) => {
          const t = cutLineText(line, project);
          return (
            <tr key={`${line.stock}:${line.length}:${line.width}:${line.thickness}`} data-testid="cutlist-line">
              <td>{t.parts}</td>
              <td>{line.quantity}</td>
              <td>{t.stock}</td>
              <td className="num">{t.cut}</td>
              <td className="num muted">{t.visible}</td>
            </tr>
          );
        })}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

`ui/SheetsTab.tsx` (each sheet drawn to scale; the drawing is decoration with a text title, so it is `aria-hidden` and the title says what it is):

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Sheet layouts (Cut list & nesting, "Outputs"): each sheet drawn to scale with its parts named.

import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { useKerf } from "./useKerf";

const WIDTH_PX = 240;

export function SheetsTab() {
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const sheets = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result.nesting.sheets);
  if (!project || !sheets) return null;
  return (
    <ol className="sheets">
      {sheets.map((sheet, i) => {
        const scale = WIDTH_PX / sheet.sheet.length;
        const name = project.stock.find((s) => s.id === sheet.stock)?.name ?? "";
        return (
          // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: sheets are numbered by position in the plan.
          <li key={i} data-testid="sheet" className="sheet">
            <p className="sheet-title">Sheet {i + 1} of {sheets.length} · {name}</p>
            <svg aria-hidden="true" width={WIDTH_PX} height={sheet.sheet.width * scale} className="sheet-drawing">
              <rect width={WIDTH_PX} height={sheet.sheet.width * scale} className="sheet-board" />
              {sheet.placements.map((p) => (
                <g key={p.instance}>
                  <rect x={p.x * scale} y={p.y * scale} width={p.length * scale} height={p.width * scale} className="sheet-part" />
                  <text x={(p.x + p.length / 2) * scale} y={(p.y + p.width / 2) * scale} className="sheet-label">{p.name}</text>
                </g>
              ))}
            </svg>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ol>
  );
}
```

(Pixel coordinates are display floats; they are computed from the engine's integers and never fed back.)

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool

          <span className="muted">{setup.parts.map((p) => `${p.name} (${p.cuts})`).join(", ")}</span>

      ))}

  );
}
```

Styles:

```css
.drawer { display: flex; flex-direction: column; height: var(--drawer-height); background: var(--color-surface); border-top: var(--border-width) solid var(--color-border); }
.drawer.tall { height: var(--drawer-height-tall); }
.tabs { display: flex; gap: var(--space-1); padding: 0 var(--space-3); border-bottom: var(--border-width) solid var(--color-border); }
.tabs [role="tab"] { border: 0; border-bottom: 2px solid transparent; border-radius: 0; background: none; color: var(--color-text-muted); padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); }
.tabs [role="tab"][aria-selected="true"] { color: var(--color-text); border-bottom-color: var(--color-accent); font-weight: var(--font-weight-strong); }
.banner { margin: 0; padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); color: var(--color-error); border-bottom: var(--border-width) solid var(--color-border); }
.tab-panel { flex: 1; overflow: auto; padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); }
.plan-table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: var(--font-size-small); }
.plan-table th { text-align: left; font-weight: var(--font-weight-normal); color: var(--color-text-muted); padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-2); }
.plan-table td { padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-2); border-top: var(--border-width) solid var(--color-border); }
.num { text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: var(--font-numeric); white-space: nowrap; }
.muted { color: var(--color-text-muted); }
```

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool

// Domain model, ".kerf file"; Review Focus 2: a damaged or newer file is refused in shop words and
// the current project is kept.

import { serializeProject } from "@kerf/core";
import { strToU8, zipSync } from "fflate";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startProject } from "./commands";
import { fileNameFor, openKerfBytes, saveKerfBytes } from "./files";

const times = { created: "2026-10-12T10:00:00.000Z", modified: "2026-10-12T11:00:00.000Z" };
const project = startProject("Bookcase", "imperial").project;

describe("files", () => {
  it("opens what it saved, byte for byte the same project", () => {
    const opened = openKerfBytes(saveKerfBytes(project, times));
    expect(opened.ok && serializeProject(opened.project)).toBe(serializeProject(project));
  });

  it("refuses bytes that are not a .kerf file, saying so plainly", () => {
    const r = openKerfBytes(new TextEncoder().encode("not a zip"));
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ ok: false });

  });

    const text = serializeProject(project).replace('"schemaVersion": 1', '"schemaVersion": 2');
    const meta = JSON.stringify({ schemaVersion: 2, appVersion: "9.0.0", ...times });
    const bytes = zipSync({ "project.json": strToU8(text), "meta.json": strToU8(meta) });
    const r = openKerfBytes(bytes);
    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!r.ok) expect(r.message).toMatch(/newer/i);
  });

  it("names the file after the project", () => {
    expect(fileNameFor(project)).toBe("Bookcase.kerf");
  });

  it("makes a safe file name from any project name", () => {
    expect(fileNameFor({ ...project, name: 'Shop/Cabinet: "v2"' })).toBe("Shop-Cabinet-v2.kerf");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `model/files.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Saving and opening .kerf files (Domain model, ".kerf file"). The engine and @kerf/io do the
// checking; this module turns their errors into one plain sentence and never throws for bad bytes.

import { CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION, type Project, ProjectFormatError } from "@kerf/core";
import { KerfFormatError, readKerf, writeKerf } from "@kerf/io";

/** The app version written into meta.json. */
export const APP_VERSION = "0.4.0";

export function saveKerfBytes(project: Project, times: { created: string; modified: string }, thumbnail?: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
  return writeKerf(project, { schemaVersion: CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION, appVersion: APP_VERSION, ...times }, thumbnail);
}

export function openKerfBytes(bytes: Uint8Array): { ok: true; project: Project } | { ok: false; message: string } {
  try {
    return { ok: true, project: readKerf(bytes).project };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof KerfFormatError || error instanceof ProjectFormatError) {
      return { ok: false, message: `This file could not be opened: ${error.message}` };
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

/** A file name from the project name, with characters file systems reject replaced. */
export function fileNameFor(project: Project): string {
  const base = project.name.replace(/["\\/:*?<>|]+/g, "-").replace(/-+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "").trim();
  return `${base || "Untitled"}.kerf`;
}
```

(Check `readKerf`'s return shape in `packages/io/src/index.ts` (`{ project, meta, thumbnail? }`) and `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION`'s export before writing. `APP_VERSION` is the app's; read it from `packages/app/package.json`'s `version` through Vite's `import.meta.env` only if a test can check it; otherwise keep the constant and bump it with releases, said in its comment.)

Add a store test: `begin` with `clockDate: () => Date.UTC(2026, 9, 12)` sets `created` to `"2026-10-12T00:00:00.000Z"` and survives a reload.

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Save and Open (design spec, "Saving"). Opening is one History entry, so it can be undone.

import { useRef } from "react";
import { fileNameFor, openKerfBytes, saveKerfBytes } from "../model/files";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

function download(bytes: Uint8Array, name: string): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([bytes], { type: "application/zip" }));
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

export function FileSection() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const hasProject = useKerf((s) => s.history !== null);
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const save = async (): Promise<void> => {
    const s = store.getState();
    const project = shownProject(s);
    if (!project) return;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const thumbnail = s.thumbnail ? await s.thumbnail() : undefined;
    download(saveKerfBytes(project, { created: s.created ?? now, modified: now }, thumbnail), fileNameFor(project));
  };

  const open = async (file: File): Promise<void> => {
    const r = openKerfBytes(new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()));
    if (r.ok) store.getState().begin({ label: `Opened ${file.name}`, project: r.project });
    else store.getState().say(r.message);
  };

  return (
    <div className="button-row">
      <button type="button" onClick={() => input.current?.click()}>
        Open…
      </button>
      <input
        ref={input}
        type="file"
        accept=".kerf"
        aria-label="Open a .kerf file"
        className="visually-hidden"
        onChange={(e) => {
          const file = e.target.files?.[0];
          if (file) void open(file);
          e.target.value = "";
        }}
      />
      <button type="button" onClick={() => void save()} disabled={!hasProject}>
        Save
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Add `<Section title="File"><FileSection /></Section>` to `StartStep` after "Size". Add `.visually-hidden { position: absolute; width: 1px; height: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip: rect(0 0 0 0); white-space: nowrap; }` to `styles.css`.

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool

// Interaction spec, "Neutral chrome": "a light theme as a setting"; Domain model, units: "Imperial
// fractions by default, metric as a setting". ADR 015, rule 4.

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startProject } from "../model/commands";
import { createStorage } from "../model/storage";
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `ui/SettingsMenu.tsx`**

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Settings in the top bar: theme (a browser preference) and units (the project's own field, so a
// change is an undoable History entry; ADR 015, rule 4).

import { useState } from "react";
import { setUnits } from "../model/commands";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "./useKerf";

export function SettingsMenu() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const theme = useKerf((s) => s.theme);
  const units = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s)?.units);
  const chooseUnits = (u: "imperial" | "metric"): void => {
    const p = shownProject(store.getState());
    if (p && p.units !== u) store.getState().apply(setUnits(p, u));
  };
  return (
    <div className="settings">
      <button type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}>
        Settings
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="settings-panel">
          <fieldset>
            <legend>Theme</legend>
            <label className="check"><input type="radio" name="theme" checked={theme === "dark"} onChange={() => store.getState().setTheme("dark")} />Dark</label>
            <label className="check"><input type="radio" name="theme" checked={theme === "light"} onChange={() => store.getState().setTheme("light")} />Light</label>
          </fieldset>
          <fieldset disabled={units === undefined}>
            <legend>Units</legend>
            <label className="check"><input type="radio" name="units" checked={units === "imperial"} onChange={() => chooseUnits("imperial")} />Inches</label>
            <label className="check"><input type="radio" name="units" checked={units === "metric"} onChange={() => chooseUnits("metric")} />Millimetres</label>
          </fieldset>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Add `<SettingsMenu />` to `TopBar` before Undo. The panel is attached under its button and closes on a second click or on Escape. Add to the component, before `return`:

```tsx
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [open]);
```

`viewport/frames.test.ts`:

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Each board is drawn where the engine places it. The right side of the 36 × 72 × 12 bookcase
// stands at x 0 to 23/32, y 0 to 12 (back to front), z 0 to 72 (Interaction spec; Task 2's test).

import { INCH, solve } from "@kerf/core";
import { loadShopProfile } from "@kerf/fixtures";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { startProject } from "../model/commands";
import { matrixOf, toScene } from "./frames";

const placements = solve(startProject("Bookcase", "imperial").project, loadShopProfile()).placements;

function apply(m: number[], p: [number, number, number]): number[] {
  return [0, 1, 2].map((r) => (m[r] ?? 0) * p[0] + (m[4 + r] ?? 0) * p[1] + (m[8 + r] ?? 0) * p[2] + (m[12 + r] ?? 0));
}

const key = (v: number[]): string => v.map((n) => n.toFixed(6)).join(",");

describe("matrixOf()", () => {
  it("puts the right side's far corner at 23/32, 72 up, 12 toward the front", () => {
    const right = placements.find((p) => p.name === "Right side");
    if (!right) throw new Error("no right side");
    const corners = [0, right.size.length].flatMap((u) => [0, right.size.width].flatMap((v) => [0, right.size.thickness].map((w) => key(apply(matrixOf(right.frame), [u / INCH, v / INCH, w / INCH])))));
    // Scene: x = project x, y = project z (up), z = −project y. Box x 0..0.71875, y 0..72, z −12..0.
    expect(new Set(corners)).toEqual(new Set([0, 0.71875].flatMap((x) => [0, 72].flatMap((y) => [-12, 0].map((z) => key([x, y, z]))))));
  });

  it("maps every board's corners onto its box", () => {
    for (const p of placements) {
      const corners = new Set([0, p.size.length].flatMap((u) => [0, p.size.width].flatMap((v) => [0, p.size.thickness].map((w) => key(apply(matrixOf(p.frame), [u / INCH, v / INCH, w / INCH]))))));
      const box = new Set([p.box.min[0], p.box.max[0]].flatMap((x) => [p.box.min[1], p.box.max[1]].flatMap((y) => [p.box.min[2], p.box.max[2]].map((z) => key(toScene([x / INCH, y / INCH, z / INCH]))))));
      expect(corners).toEqual(box);
    }
  });
});
```

`viewport/camera.test.ts`:

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Interaction spec, navigation "matches SketchUp"; Shift+Z zooms to fit. Camera numbers are display
// floats, so they are compared to a hand calculation within 0.1.

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { cameraPosition, fitView, orbit, zoom } from "./camera";

describe("fitView()", () => {
  it("frames the 36 × 72 × 12 bookcase from its centre", () => {
    const v = fitView([0, 0, -12], [36, 72, 0]);
    expect(v.target).toEqual([18, 36, -6]);
    // Half the diagonal: √(36² + 72² + 12²) / 2 = √6624 / 2 = 40.694; ÷ sin(17.5°) = 0.30071 → 135.33.
    expect(v.distance).toBeCloseTo(135.33, 1);
  });

  it("looks from the front: the camera is on the front side (scene −z) and above", () => {
    const p = cameraPosition(fitView([0, 0, -12], [36, 72, 0]));
    expect(p[2]).toBeLessThan(-6);
    expect(p[1]).toBeGreaterThan(36);
  });
});

describe("orbit()", () => {
  it("never tips past straight up or straight down", () => {
    const v = orbit(fitView([0, 0, 0], [1, 1, 1]), 0, -100_000);
    expect(Math.abs(v.pitch)).toBeLessThan(Math.PI / 2);
  });
});

describe("zoom()", () => {
  it("moves closer when the wheel turns forward", () => {
    const v = fitView([0, 0, 0], [10, 10, 10]);
    expect(zoom(v, -100).distance).toBeLessThan(v.distance);
  });
});
```

```ts
// Domain model, solve step 7: "Display mesh … cached by definition hash". ADR 015, rule 10: a shape
// that cannot be built says why.

import type { PartShape } from "@kerf/core";
import { initGeometry } from "@kerf/geometry";
import { beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { meshFor } from "./meshes";

const shelf: PartShape = { definition: "a", name: "Shelf", length: 7_124_700, width: 2_387_600, thickness: 146_050, removals: [] };

describe("meshFor()", () => {
  beforeAll(initGeometry);

  it("builds the same mesh once for two shapes with the same content", () => {
    const a = meshFor(shelf);
    const b = meshFor({ ...shelf, definition: "b" });
    expect(a.ok && b.ok && a.mesh === b.mesh).toBe(true);
  });

  it("says why a shape cannot be built instead of throwing", () => {
    const bad = { ...shelf, removals: [{ kind: "nonsense" }] } as unknown as PartShape;
    expect(meshFor(bad)).toMatchObject({ ok: false });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `frames.ts` and `camera.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// From the engine's placements to the 3D scene. The project frame is x left-right, y back to front,
// z up; the scene is y up. (x, y, z) → (x, z, −y) is a rotation (determinant +1), so only mirrored
// boards get a flipped matrix, which three.js draws correctly. Scene units are inches (display only).

import { INCH, type Len, type PlacedPart, type Vec3 } from "@kerf/core";

export function toScene(v: readonly [number, number, number]): [number, number, number] {
  return [v[0], v[2], -v[1]];
}

/** A scene point (inches) back in project units, snapped to the grid; the only float → integer step. */
export function fromScene(v: readonly [number, number, number], grid: Len): Vec3 {
  const snap = (inches: number): Len => Math.round((inches * INCH) / grid) * grid;
  return [snap(v[0]), snap(-v[2]), snap(v[1])];
}

/** The board's placement as a column-major 4x4 matrix, from shape inches to scene inches. */
export function matrixOf(frame: PlacedPart["frame"]): number[] {
  const x = toScene(frame.x);
  const y = toScene(frame.y);
  const z = toScene(frame.z);
  const o = toScene(frame.origin).map((n) => n / INCH);
  return [x[0], x[1], x[2], 0, y[0], y[1], y[2], 0, z[0], z[1], z[2], 0, o[0] ?? 0, o[1] ?? 0, o[2] ?? 0, 1];
}
```

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// SketchUp-style navigation (Interaction spec, "3D tools"): orbit about a target, pan, zoom, and
// zoom to fit. A view is a target, a distance and two angles; positions follow from them.

export interface View {
  target: [number, number, number];
  distance: number;
  /** Turn about the vertical, in radians; 0 looks from the front (scene −z). */
  yaw: number;
  /** Tilt, in radians; positive looks down from above. */
  pitch: number;
}

export const FOV_DEGREES = 35;
const DEFAULT_YAW = -0.6; // from the front-right
const DEFAULT_PITCH = 0.35; // a little above
const RADIANS_PER_PIXEL = 0.01;
const PITCH_LIMIT = Math.PI / 2 - 0.05;

export function fitView(min: [number, number, number], max: [number, number, number]): View {
  const target: [number, number, number] = [(min[0] + max[0]) / 2, (min[1] + max[1]) / 2, (min[2] + max[2]) / 2];
  const radius = Math.hypot(max[0] - min[0], max[1] - min[1], max[2] - min[2]) / 2;
  const distance = Math.max(radius, 1) / Math.sin(((FOV_DEGREES / 2) * Math.PI) / 180);
  return { target, distance, yaw: DEFAULT_YAW, pitch: DEFAULT_PITCH };
}

export function cameraPosition(v: View): [number, number, number] {
  const c = Math.cos(v.pitch);
  return [v.target[0] + v.distance * Math.sin(v.yaw) * c, v.target[1] + v.distance * Math.sin(v.pitch), v.target[2] - v.distance * Math.cos(v.yaw) * c];
}

export function orbit(v: View, dxPx: number, dyPx: number): View {
  const pitch = Math.min(PITCH_LIMIT, Math.max(-PITCH_LIMIT, v.pitch - dyPx * RADIANS_PER_PIXEL));
  return { ...v, yaw: v.yaw - dxPx * RADIANS_PER_PIXEL, pitch };
}

/** Moves the target with the pointer, scaled so the model follows the cursor. */
export function pan(v: View, dxPx: number, dyPx: number, heightPx: number): View {
  const perPixel = (2 * v.distance * Math.tan(((FOV_DEGREES / 2) * Math.PI) / 180)) / Math.max(heightPx, 1);
  const right: [number, number, number] = [-Math.cos(v.yaw), 0, -Math.sin(v.yaw)];
  const up: [number, number, number] = [-Math.sin(v.yaw) * Math.sin(v.pitch), Math.cos(v.pitch), Math.cos(v.yaw) * Math.sin(v.pitch)];
  const t = v.target.map((n, i) => n - (right[i] ?? 0) * dxPx * perPixel + (up[i] ?? 0) * dyPx * perPixel) as [number, number, number];
  return { ...v, target: t };
}

export function zoom(v: View, wheelDelta: number): View {
  return { ...v, distance: Math.min(5000, Math.max(1, v.distance * 1.1 ** (wheelDelta / 100))) };
}
```

Add to `camera.test.ts` (with yaw 0 the camera sits at scene −z looking toward +z, so the viewer's right is scene −x; dragging right moves the model right on screen, so the target moves the other way, toward scene +x):

```ts

  it("moves the target toward the viewer's left when the pointer drags right", () => {
    const v = { ...fitView([0, 0, 0], [10, 10, 10]), yaw: 0, pitch: 0 };
    expect(pan(v, 100, 0, 500).target[0]).toBeGreaterThan(v.target[0]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `meshes.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Display meshes for the viewport (Domain model, solve step 7), built by @kerf/geometry and kept by
// content, so a shape is built once however many copies are drawn and however often the project is
// solved. Outputs never read these (ADR 013, D9).

import type { PartShape } from "@kerf/core";
import { buildDisplayMesh, type DisplayMesh, MeshError } from "@kerf/geometry";

export type MeshResult = { ok: true; mesh: DisplayMesh } | { ok: false; reason: string };

/** Most meshes kept; beyond this the oldest is dropped, so a long session cannot grow without end. */
const CACHE_LIMIT = 500;
const cache = new Map<string, MeshResult>();

/** The shape's display mesh, or why it cannot be made (ADR 015, rule 10). Call after initGeometry(). */
export function meshFor(shape: PartShape): MeshResult {
  const key = JSON.stringify([shape.length, shape.width, shape.thickness, shape.removals]);
  const found = cache.get(key);
  if (found) return found;
  let result: MeshResult;
  try {
    result = { ok: true, mesh: buildDisplayMesh({ length: shape.length, width: shape.width, thickness: shape.thickness }, shape.removals) };
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof MeshError)) throw error;
    result = { ok: false, reason: error.message };
  }
  if (cache.size >= CACHE_LIMIT) cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value as string);
  cache.set(key, result);
  return result;
}
```

The cache is module state; that is allowed outside `core`, and because each entry is a pure function of its key it can only return a remembered answer, never a wrong one. Add this sentence to the header comment.

`viewport/Viewport.tsx`:

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The model (Interaction spec, "Viewport (center)"): every placed board, drawn with its cuts, wood-
// toned by stock, red when the engine reports an error on it, lit when selected, its grain shown on
// hover; SketchUp navigation. Picks go to the active tool through tools/dispatch.ts.

import type { Id, PartShape, PlacedPart, Project } from "@kerf/core";
import { initGeometry } from "@kerf/geometry";
import { Canvas, type ThreeEvent, useThree } from "@react-three/fiber";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { BufferAttribute, BufferGeometry, Matrix4 } from "three";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { dispatchPointer } from "../tools/dispatch";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "../ui/useKerf";
import { cameraPosition, FOV_DEGREES, fitView, orbit, pan, type View, zoom } from "./camera";
import { matrixOf, toScene } from "./frames";
import { meshFor } from "./meshes";

const INCHES = (n: number): number => n / 203_200;

/** Reads a colour token so the scene follows the theme. */
function token(name: string): string {
  return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name).trim();
}

/** The plain blank as triangles (ADR 015, rules 8 and 10), when a shape is withheld or fails. */
function blankGeometry(placed: PlacedPart): BufferGeometry {
  const [l, w, t] = [placed.size.length, placed.size.width, placed.size.thickness].map(INCHES) as [number, number, number];
  const g = new BufferGeometry();
  g.setAttribute("position", new BufferAttribute(new Float32Array([0, 0, 0, l, 0, 0, l, w, 0, 0, w, 0, 0, 0, t, l, 0, t, l, w, t, 0, w, t]), 3));
  g.setIndex([0, 2, 1, 0, 3, 2, 4, 5, 6, 4, 6, 7, 0, 1, 5, 0, 5, 4, 1, 2, 6, 1, 6, 5, 2, 3, 7, 2, 7, 6, 3, 0, 4, 3, 4, 7]);
  g.computeVertexNormals();
  return g;
}

function geometryOf(shape: PartShape | undefined, placed: PlacedPart, ready: boolean): BufferGeometry {
  const built = ready && shape ? meshFor(shape) : undefined;
  if (!built?.ok) return blankGeometry(placed);
  const g = new BufferGeometry();
  g.setAttribute("position", new BufferAttribute(built.mesh.positions, 3));
  g.setIndex(new BufferAttribute(built.mesh.indices, 1));
  g.computeVertexNormals();
  return g;
}

interface BoardProps {
  placed: PlacedPart;
  shape: PartShape | undefined;
  ready: boolean;
  wood: "sheet" | "solid";
  broken: boolean;
  selected: boolean;
}

function Board({ placed, shape, ready, wood, broken, selected }: BoardProps) {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const geometry = useMemo(() => geometryOf(shape, placed, ready), [shape, placed, ready]);
  const matrix = useMemo(() => new Matrix4().fromArray(matrixOf(placed.frame)), [placed.frame]);
  const color = broken ? token("--color-error") : token(wood === "solid" ? "--color-wood-solid" : "--color-wood-sheet");
  const send = (kind: "down" | "move" | "up") => (e: ThreeEvent<PointerEvent>) => {
    if (kind === "down" && e.button !== 0) return;
    e.stopPropagation();
    dispatchPointer(store, kind, e, placed);
  };
  return (
    <mesh
      geometry={geometry}
      matrix={matrix}
      matrixAutoUpdate={false}
      onPointerDown={send("down")}
      onPointerMove={send("move")}
      onPointerUp={send("up")}
      onPointerOver={() => store.getState().hover(placed.instance)}
      onPointerOut={() => store.getState().hover(null)}
    >
      <meshStandardMaterial color={color} emissive={selected ? token("--color-accent") : "#000000"} emissiveIntensity={selected ? 0.35 : 0} />
    </mesh>
  );
}

/** A line along the hovered board's length, on its show face: the grain direction. */
function GrainLine({ placed }: { placed: PlacedPart }) {
  const geometry = useMemo(() => {
    const m = matrixOf(placed.frame);
    const v = INCHES(placed.size.width) / 2;
    const w = INCHES(placed.size.thickness) + 0.02;
    const at = (u: number): number[] => [0, 1, 2].map((r) => (m[r] ?? 0) * u + (m[4 + r] ?? 0) * v + (m[8 + r] ?? 0) * w + (m[12 + r] ?? 0));
    const g = new BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute("position", new BufferAttribute(new Float32Array([...at(0), ...at(INCHES(placed.size.length))]), 3));
    return g;
  }, [placed]);
  return (
    <lineSegments geometry={geometry}>
      <lineBasicMaterial color={token("--color-focus")} />
    </lineSegments>
  );
}

/** Lets the view's picture be the saved file's thumbnail (design spec, "Saving"). */
function ThumbnailSource() {
  const { gl } = useThree();
  const store = useKerfStore();
  useEffect(() => {
    store.getState().setThumbnailSource(
      () =>
        new Promise((resolve) => {
          gl.domElement.toBlob((blob) => {
            if (!blob) {
              resolve(undefined);
              return;
            }
            blob.arrayBuffer().then(
              (bytes) => resolve(new Uint8Array(bytes)),
              () => resolve(undefined),
            );
          }, "image/png");
        }),
    );
    return () => store.getState().setThumbnailSource(null);
  }, [gl, store]);
  return null;
}

function Navigation({ view, setView }: { view: View; setView(v: View): void }) {
  const { camera, gl, size } = useThree();
  const mode = useKerf((s) => s.navigation);
  useEffect(() => {
    camera.position.set(...cameraPosition(view));
    camera.lookAt(...view.target);
  }, [camera, view]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = gl.domElement;
    let last: { x: number; y: number; panning: boolean } | null = null;
    const down = (e: PointerEvent): void => {
      const left = e.button === 0 && mode !== null;
      if (e.button === 1 || left) last = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, panning: e.shiftKey || (left && mode === "pan") };
    };
    const move = (e: PointerEvent): void => {
      if (!last) return;
      const dx = e.clientX - last.x;
      const dy = e.clientY - last.y;
      last = { ...last, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
      setView(last.panning ? pan(view, dx, dy, size.height) : orbit(view, dx, dy));
    };
    const up = (): void => {
      last = null;
    };
    const wheel = (e: WheelEvent): void => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setView(zoom(view, e.deltaY));
    };
    el.addEventListener("pointerdown", down);
    window.addEventListener("pointermove", move);
    window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
    el.addEventListener("wheel", wheel, { passive: false });
    return () => {
      el.removeEventListener("pointerdown", down);
      window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move);
      window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up);
      el.removeEventListener("wheel", wheel);
    };
  }, [gl, view, setView, size.height, mode]);
  return null;
}

function sceneBounds(placements: PlacedPart[]): [[number, number, number], [number, number, number]] {
  const pts = placements.flatMap((p) => [toScene(p.box.min), toScene(p.box.max)].map((v) => v.map(INCHES)));
  const min = [0, 1, 2].map((i) => Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p[i] ?? 0))) as [number, number, number];
  const max = [0, 1, 2].map((i) => Math.max(...pts.map((p) => p[i] ?? 0))) as [number, number, number];
  return [min, max];
}

function woodOf(project: Project, part: Id): "sheet" | "solid" {
  const stock = project.stock.find((s) => s.id === project.parts.find((d) => d.id === part)?.stock);
  return stock?.kind === "sheet" ? "sheet" : "solid";
}

export function Viewport() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const project = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s));
  const result = useKerf((s) => s.solved?.result);
  const selection = useKerf((s) => s.selection);
  const hovered = useKerf((s) => s.hovered);
  const fitRequest = useKerf((s) => s.fitRequest);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const [view, setView] = useState<View | null>(null);
  const placements = useMemo(() => result?.placements ?? [], [result]);
  const hasBoards = placements.length > 0;

  useEffect(() => {
    initGeometry().then(
      () => setReady(true),
      (e: unknown) => store.getState().say(`The 3D view could not start (${e instanceof Error ? e.message : "unknown reason"}), so boards are shown as plain blanks.`),
    );
  }, [store]);

  // Fit when boards first appear and whenever Shift+Z asks. Later edits keep the user's view, so
  // the placements are read here but deliberately not watched.
  // biome-ignore lint/correctness/useExhaustiveDependencies: refitting on every edit would fight the user.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasBoards) setView(fitView(...sceneBounds(placements)));
  }, [hasBoards, fitRequest]);

  if (!project || !result) return <p className="model-empty">Start a project to see it here.</p>;
  const shapes = new Map(result.shapes.map((x) => [x.definition, x]));
  const broken = new Set(result.diagnostics.flatMap((d) => (d.severity === "error" && d.part ? [d.part] : [])));
  const selected = new Set(selection.kind === "boards" ? selection.instances : []);
  const hoveredBoard = placements.find((p) => p.instance === hovered);

  return (
    <Canvas camera={{ fov: FOV_DEGREES, near: 0.1, far: 10_000 }} gl={{ preserveDrawingBuffer: true }} onPointerMissed={(e) => dispatchPointer(store, "down", e, null)}>
      <hemisphereLight intensity={0.9} groundColor="#3a3d42" />
      <directionalLight position={[60, 120, -80]} intensity={1.4} />
      <ThumbnailSource />
      {view && <Navigation view={view} setView={setView} />}
      {placements.map((p) => (
        <Board key={p.instance} placed={p} shape={shapes.get(p.shape)} ready={ready} wood={woodOf(project, p.part)} broken={broken.has(p.part)} selected={selected.has(p.instance)} />
      ))}
      {hoveredBoard && <GrainLine placed={hoveredBoard} />}
    </Canvas>
  );
}
```

A board drawn as a blank because its mesh failed gets its reason in the inspector: in `BoardInspector`, when `meshFor(shape)` is not `ok` for the first selected board's shape, show `The 3D view shows this board without its cuts: {reason}` as a warning line. Add a `BoardInspector` test with a shape carrying an unknown removal.

`tools/dispatch.ts` (the one place a three.js event becomes a `ModelPointer`):

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Turns a pointer event on the model into what a tool needs: the board, the point on the grid and
// the surface's direction, in project terms. The only place scene floats become project integers.

import { gridSize, type PlacedPart, type Vec3 } from "@kerf/core";
import type { ThreeEvent } from "@react-three/fiber";
import { type KerfStore, shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { fromScene } from "../viewport/frames";
import { handlerFor, type ModelHit } from "./pointer";

/** A scene direction back to the signed project axis it is closest to. */
function axisOf(x: number, y: number, z: number): Vec3 {
  const project = [x, -z, y];
  const sizes = project.map(Math.abs);
  const k = sizes.indexOf(Math.max(...sizes));
  return [0, 1, 2].map((i) => (i === k ? Math.sign(project[i] ?? 0) : 0)) as unknown as Vec3;
}

export function dispatchPointer(store: KerfStore, kind: "down" | "move" | "up", e: ThreeEvent<PointerEvent> | MouseEvent, placed: PlacedPart | null): void {
  const s = store.getState();
  const project = shownProject(s);
  if (!project) return;
  let hit: ModelHit | null = null;
  if (placed && "point" in e && e.face) {
    const n = e.face.normal.clone().transformDirection(e.object.matrixWorld);
    hit = { placed, point: fromScene([e.point.x, e.point.y, e.point.z], gridSize(project.units)), normal: axisOf(n.x, n.y, n.z) };
  }
  handlerFor(s.tool).pointer?.({ kind, hit, shift: e.shiftKey, ctrl: e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey }, store);
}
```

`tools/pointer.ts` is Task 25's file. Create it in this task with the `ModelHit`, `ModelPointer` and `ToolHandler` types, the `select` handler and the handler table exactly as Task 25, Step 3 shows them, so this task's commit compiles; Task 25 then adds the tool row and keys.

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The escape-hatch tools (Interaction spec, "3D tools" table) and their SketchUp keys.

import type { ToolId } from "../model/store";

export const TOOLS: ReadonlyArray<{ id: ToolId; label: string; key: string }> = [
  { id: "select", label: "Select", key: "Space" },
  { id: "board", label: "Board", key: "B" },
  { id: "pushPull", label: "Push/Pull", key: "P" },
  { id: "move", label: "Move", key: "M" },
  { id: "join", label: "Join", key: "J" },
  { id: "edge", label: "Edge profile", key: "F" },
  { id: "tape", label: "Tape", key: "T" },
];

/** The tool a key picks. R is Board too (SketchUp's rectangle); E is left unassigned on purpose. */
export function toolForKey(key: string): ToolId | undefined {
  if (key === " ") return "select";
  const k = key.toLowerCase();
  if (k === "r") return "board";
  return TOOLS.find((t) => t.key.toLowerCase() === k && t.key !== "Space")?.id;
}
```

`tools/pointer.ts` (the handler table starts with Select; Tasks 26-31 add theirs):

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// What the model view tells a tool: which board was hit, where (project units, on the grid) and
// which way the surface faces (a project axis). Each tool turns this into named changes.

import type { PlacedPart, Vec3 } from "@kerf/core";
import type { KerfState, KerfStore, ToolId } from "../model/store";

export interface ModelHit {
  placed: PlacedPart;
  point: Vec3;
  /** The surface's outward direction: a signed project axis. */
  normal: Vec3;
}

export interface ModelPointer {
  kind: "down" | "move" | "up";
  hit: ModelHit | null;
  shift: boolean;
  ctrl: boolean;
}

export interface ToolHandler {
  pointer?(e: ModelPointer, store: KerfStore): void;
  /** Takes text typed in the Measurements box; returns why it was refused, or null. */
  measure?(text: string, store: KerfStore): string | null;
  /** What the Measurements box asks for now. */
  prompt?(state: KerfState): string;
}

const select: ToolHandler = {
  pointer(e, store) {
    if (e.kind !== "down") return;
    const s = store.getState();
    if (!e.hit) return s.select({ kind: "none" });
    const now = s.selection.kind === "boards" ? s.selection.instances : [];
    s.select({ kind: "boards", instances: e.shift ? [...new Set([...now, e.hit.placed.instance])] : [e.hit.placed.instance] });
  },
};

/**
 * Every tool's handler, written out so the wiring is visible. A tool whose task has not landed yet
 * selects; Tasks 27-31 replace their entry and import their handler here. The Edge tool works from
 * the inspector (Task 26), so clicking boards while it is active picks them.
 */
const HANDLERS: Record<ToolId, ToolHandler> = {
  select,
  board: select,
  pushPull: select,
  move: select,
  join: select,
  edge: select,
  tape: select,
};

export function handlerFor(tool: ToolId): ToolHandler {
  return HANDLERS[tool];
}
```

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The Measurements box (Interaction spec: "the type-anywhere Measurements box work[s] the same" as
// SketchUp): typed sizes go to the active tool.

import { useId, useState } from "react";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "../ui/useKerf";
import { handlerFor } from "./pointer";

export function Measurements() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const id = useId();
  const tool = useKerf((s) => s.tool);
  const prompt = useKerf((s) => handlerFor(s.tool).prompt?.(s) ?? "");
  const [text, setText] = useState("");
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const submit = (): void => {
    const handler = handlerFor(tool);
    const refused = handler.measure ? handler.measure(text, store) : "This tool takes no typed size.";
    setProblem(refused);
    if (!refused) setText("");
  };
  return (
    <div className="measurements">
      <label htmlFor={id}>Measurements</label>
      <input id={id} value={text} placeholder={prompt} aria-invalid={problem !== null} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && submit()} />
      {problem && <span className="field-problem">{problem}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `tools/edge.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Edge profile (Interaction spec, "3D tools": "Add a roundover to edges, using bits from the shop
// profile"). Shelves the form makes keep following it: their roundover is the form's (ADR 015, D3).

import { type EdgeRef, formatLength, type Len, type PlacedPart, type Project } from "@kerf/core";
import { addRoundover, type Edit, setShelfRoundover } from "../model/commands";

/** The front edge on both faces (Operation specs, bookcase: edge1/face0 and edge1/face1). */
export const BOTH_FRONT_EDGES: EdgeRef[] = ["edge1/face0", "edge1/face1"];

const EDGE_WORDS: Record<string, string> = { edge1: "Front edge", edge0: "Back edge", end0: "First end", end1: "Second end" };
const FACE_WORDS: Record<string, string> = { face1: "show face", face0: "inside face" };

/** "edge1/face1" → "Front edge, show face" (Interaction spec, "Shop language"). */
export function edgeName(edge: EdgeRef): string {
  const [e, f] = edge.split("/") as [string, string];
  return `${EDGE_WORDS[e]}, ${FACE_WORDS[f]}`;
}

const isFormShelf = (p: PlacedPart): boolean => p.source?.role === "shelf" && !p.detached;
const sameEdges = (a: EdgeRef[], b: EdgeRef[]): boolean => a.length === b.length && a.every((x) => b.includes(x));

/** Rounds `edges` of the picked boards (null: no roundover). One History entry. */
export function roundEdges(project: Project, placed: PlacedPart[], edges: EdgeRef[], radius: Len | null): Edit {
  const first = placed[0];
  if (first && placed.every(isFormShelf) && sameEdges(edges, BOTH_FRONT_EDGES) && first.source) {
    return setShelfRoundover(project, first.source.structure, radius);
  }
  if (radius === null) {
    const parts = new Set(placed.map((p) => p.part));
    const next = { ...project, parts: project.parts.map((d) => (parts.has(d.id) ? { ...d, ops: d.ops.filter((op) => op.type !== "roundover") } : d)) };
    return { project: next, label: placed.length === 1 && first ? `No roundover on ${first.name}` : `No roundover on ${placed.length} boards` };
  }
  // Copies of one board share its part, so each part gets the roundover once.
  const byPart = [...new Map(placed.map((p) => [p.part, p])).values()];
  let next = project;
  for (const p of byPart) next = addRoundover(next, p, edges, radius).project;
  const label = placed.length === 1 && first ? `Roundover ${formatLength(radius, project.units)} on ${first.name}` : `Roundover ${formatLength(radius, project.units)} on ${placed.length} boards`;
  return { project: next, label };
}
```

Add the test for that: append a second instance of the Top (same `def`, a new id) to the project, take the Top over with `detachForEdit`, then round both copies together; the Top part carries one roundover operation.

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The Edge profile panel: a radius from the shop's roundover bits, then both front edges or the
// edges ticked by name.

import { type EdgeRef, formatLength, type PlacedPart } from "@kerf/core";
import { useId, useState } from "react";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Section } from "../ui/Section";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "../ui/useKerf";
import { BOTH_FRONT_EDGES, edgeName, roundEdges } from "./edge";

const ALL_EDGES: EdgeRef[] = ["edge1/face1", "edge1/face0", "edge0/face1", "edge0/face0", "end0/face1", "end0/face0", "end1/face1", "end1/face0"];

export function EdgePanel({ placed }: { placed: PlacedPart[] }) {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const radiusId = useId();
  const shop = useKerf((s) => s.shop);
  const bits = shop.tools.flatMap((t) => (t.type === "router" ? t.roundoverBits : []));
  const units = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s)?.units ?? "imperial");
  const [radius, setRadius] = useState<number | null>(bits[0] ?? null);
  const [ticked, setTicked] = useState<EdgeRef[]>([]);
  const round = (edges: EdgeRef[]): void => {
    const p = shownProject(store.getState());
    if (p) store.getState().apply(roundEdges(p, placed, edges, radius));
  };
  return (
    <Section title="Roundover">
      <div className="field">
        <label htmlFor={radiusId}>Radius</label>
        <select id={radiusId} value={radius ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setRadius(e.target.value === "" ? null : Number(e.target.value))}>
          <option value="">None</option>
          {bits.map((b) => (
            <option key={b} value={b}>
              {formatLength(b, units)}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      {bits.length === 0 && <p className="muted">Your shop has no roundover bits. Add them in Start, Your shop.</p>}
      <button type="button" onClick={() => round(BOTH_FRONT_EDGES)}>
        Both front edges
      </button>
      <fieldset className="edge-list">
        <legend>Or pick edges</legend>
        {ALL_EDGES.map((edge) => (
          <label key={edge} className="check">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={ticked.includes(edge)} onChange={(e) => setTicked(e.target.checked ? [...ticked, edge] : ticked.filter((x) => x !== edge))} />
            {edgeName(edge)}
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <button type="button" disabled={ticked.length === 0} onClick={() => round(ticked)}>
        Round the ticked edges
      </button>
    </Section>
  );
}
```

The section is titled "Roundover", not "Edge profile": a section title is a button, and "Edge profile" would collide with the tool button "Edge profile F". `bits` is computed from the stored `shop` rather than in the selector, because a selector that builds a new array on every call makes the screen redraw forever (Task 14, `useKerf`).

```ts
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.message);

    const after = solve(r.edit.project, shop).placements.find((p) => p.name === "Top")?.box;

    expect(r.ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it("finds the second end from its outward direction", () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `tools/push-pull.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Push/Pull (Interaction spec, "3D tools"): change a board's length or width from one end or edge;
// thickness belongs to the stock. A board the form made is taken over first, with a notice.

import { formatLength, type Len, type PlacedPart, type Project, solveModel, type Vec3 } from "@kerf/core";
import { detachForEdit, type EditResult, instanceOf } from "../model/commands";
import { literalFor } from "../model/size";
import type { KerfStore } from "../model/store";
import type { ToolHandler } from "./pointer";

export type BoardFace = "end0" | "end1" | "edge0" | "edge1";

const same = (a: Vec3, b: Vec3): boolean => a[0] === b[0] && a[1] === b[1] && a[2] === b[2];
const negated = (v: Vec3): Vec3 => [-v[0], -v[1], -v[2]];

/** Which face of the board an outward direction belongs to. */
export function faceOf(placed: PlacedPart, normal: Vec3): BoardFace | "thickness" {
  const { x, y } = placed.frame;
  if (same(normal, x)) return "end1";
  if (same(normal, negated(x))) return "end0";
  if (same(normal, y)) return "edge1";
  if (same(normal, negated(y))) return "edge0";
  return "thickness";
}

const WORDS: Record<BoardFace, [string, string]> = {
  end0: ["Lengthened", "Shortened"],
  end1: ["Lengthened", "Shortened"],
  edge0: ["Widened", "Narrowed"],
  edge1: ["Widened", "Narrowed"],
};

/** Moves one end or edge outward by `distance` (inward when negative). */
export function pushPull(project: Project, placed: PlacedPart, face: BoardFace, distance: Len): EditResult {
  const taken = detachForEdit(project, placed);
  const inst = instanceOf(taken.project, placed);
  const def = inst && taken.project.parts.find((d) => d.id === inst.def);
  if (!inst || !def) return { ok: false, message: `${placed.name} could not be found to change.` };

  const along = face.startsWith("end") ? placed.frame.x : placed.frame.y;
  const k = along.findIndex((n) => n !== 0) as 0 | 1 | 2;
  const key = (["x", "y", "z"] as const)[k];
  const atLowEnd = face.endsWith("0") ? (along[k] ?? 0) > 0 : (along[k] ?? 0) < 0;
  const d = literalFor(Math.abs(distance), project.units);
  const grow = distance >= 0 ? "+" : "-";
  const dimension = face.startsWith("end") ? "length" : "width";

  const nextDef = { ...def, [dimension]: `(${def[dimension]}) ${grow} ${d}` };
  const nextInst = atLowEnd ? { ...inst, position: { ...inst.position, [key]: `(${inst.position[key]}) ${grow === "+" ? "-" : "+"} ${d}` } } : inst;
  const next: Project = {
    ...taken.project,
    parts: taken.project.parts.map((x) => (x.id === def.id ? nextDef : x)),
    instances: taken.project.instances.map((x) => (x.id === inst.id ? nextInst : x)),
  };

  const problem = solveModel(next).diagnostics.find((x) => x.severity === "error" && x.part === def.id);
  if (problem) return { ok: false, message: problem.message };
  const [more, less] = WORDS[face];
  return { ok: true, edit: { project: next, label: `${distance >= 0 ? more : less} ${placed.name} by ${formatLength(Math.abs(distance), project.units)}` } };
}

/** How far a drag travelled along a face's outward direction (exact: points are on the grid). */
export function travelAlong(from: Vec3, to: Vec3, normal: Vec3): Len {
  return (to[0] - from[0]) * normal[0] + (to[1] - from[1]) * normal[1] + (to[2] - from[2]) * normal[2];
}

/** History's current project: drags and typed sizes are measured from what was recorded. */
function recorded(store: KerfStore): Project | null {
  const h = store.getState().history;
  return h?.entries[h.at]?.project ?? null;
}

/** Applies one Push/Pull, with the take-over notice when the form made the board. */
function finish(store: KerfStore, placed: PlacedPart, edit: Edit): void {
  const s = store.getState();
  s.apply(edit, placed.source && !placed.detached ? `${placed.name} is now custom; the form won't change it.` : undefined);
  s.setPending(null);
}

/** Click an end or edge, then drag (previewed, recorded on release) or type a distance. */
export const pushPullTool: ToolHandler = {
  pointer(e, store) {
    const s = store.getState();
    const pending = s.pending?.tool === "pushPull" ? s.pending : null;
    const project = recorded(store);
    if (!project) return;
    if (e.kind === "down") {
      if (!e.hit) return;
      if (faceOf(e.hit.placed, e.hit.normal) === "thickness") {
        s.say("A board's thickness is set by its stock. Pick an end or an edge.");
        return;
      }
      s.setPending({ tool: "pushPull", hit: e.hit, dragged: null });
      return;
    }
    if (!pending || !e.hit) return;
    const face = faceOf(pending.hit.placed, pending.hit.normal) as BoardFace;
    const r = pushPull(project, pending.hit.placed, face, travelAlong(pending.hit.point, e.hit.point, pending.hit.normal));
    if (e.kind === "move" && r.ok && r.edit.label !== pending.dragged) {
      s.preview(r.edit.project);
      s.setPending({ ...pending, dragged: r.edit.label });
    }
    if (e.kind === "up" && pending.dragged !== null && r.ok) finish(store, pending.hit.placed, r.edit);
  },
  prompt: (s) => (s.pending?.tool === "pushPull" ? 'Distance, e.g. 1"' : "Click an end or edge"),
  measure(text, store) {
    const pending = store.getState().pending;
    if (pending?.tool !== "pushPull") return "Click an end or edge of a board first.";
    const project = recorded(store);
    if (!project) return "Start a project first.";
    const read = readLength(text, project);
    if (!read.ok) return read.message;
    const r = pushPull(project, pending.hit.placed, faceOf(pending.hit.placed, pending.hit.normal) as BoardFace, read.value);
    if (!r.ok) return r.message;
    finish(store, pending.hit.placed, r.edit);
    return null;
  },
};
```

Imports for the tool: `type Edit` from `../model/commands`, `readLength` from `../model/size`, `type KerfStore` from `../model/store`. Change the `Pending` push/pull member (Task 25) to `{ tool: "pushPull"; hit: ModelHit; dragged: string | null }`: `dragged` is the last previewed label, so a drag that ends where it started records nothing. Set `HANDLERS.pushPull = pushPullTool` in `pointer.ts`. Add to `push-pull.test.ts`:

```ts

  it("measures a drag of 1\" outward along +x", () => {
    expect(travelAlong([0, 0, 0], [IN, 5, 7], [1, 0, 0])).toBe(IN);
  });

  it("measures a drag inward as negative", () => {
    expect(travelAlong([IN, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0], [1, 0, 0])).toBe(-IN);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `tools/move.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Move / copy (Interaction spec, "3D tools": Ctrl copies, Shift makes a mirrored copy; axis locks
// and typed distances). Boards the form made are taken over first.

import { formatLength, type Instance, type PlacedPart, type Project, type Vec3 } from "@kerf/core";
import { detachForEdit, type Edit, instanceOf } from "../model/commands";
import { newId } from "../model/ids";
import { literalFor } from "../model/size";

export type MoveMode = "move" | "copy" | "mirror";
const AXES = ["x", "y", "z"] as const;

/** Only the largest direction of a drag, or only the locked axis (SketchUp's inference lock). */
export function lockedDelta(from: Vec3, to: Vec3, axis: 0 | 1 | 2 | null): Vec3 {
  const d = [to[0] - from[0], to[1] - from[1], to[2] - from[2]];
  const k = axis ?? (d.map(Math.abs).indexOf(Math.max(...d.map(Math.abs))) as 0 | 1 | 2);
  return [0, 1, 2].map((i) => (i === k ? (d[i] ?? 0) : 0)) as unknown as Vec3;
}

function shifted(inst: Instance, delta: Vec3, units: Project["units"]): Instance["position"] {
  const out = { ...inst.position };
  for (const [i, key] of AXES.entries()) {
    const d = delta[i] ?? 0;
    if (d !== 0) out[key] = `(${inst.position[key]}) ${d > 0 ? "+" : "-"} ${literalFor(Math.abs(d), units)}`;
  }
  return out;
}

export function moveBoards(project: Project, placed: PlacedPart[], delta: Vec3, mode: MoveMode): Edit {
  let next = project;
  const added: Instance[] = [];
  for (const p of placed) {
    next = detachForEdit(next, p).project;
    const inst = instanceOf(next, p);
    if (!inst) continue;
    if (mode === "move") {
      const position = shifted(inst, delta, project.units);
      next = { ...next, instances: next.instances.map((x) => (x.id === inst.id ? { ...x, position } : x)) };
    } else {
      added.push({ ...inst, id: newId(), name: `${inst.name ?? p.name} copy`, position: shifted(inst, delta, project.units), mirrored: mode === "mirror" ? !inst.mirrored : inst.mirrored });
    }
  }
  next = { ...next, instances: [...next.instances, ...added] };
  const who = placed.length === 1 ? (placed[0]?.name ?? "board") : `${placed.length} boards`;
  const distance = Math.abs(delta[0]) + Math.abs(delta[1]) + Math.abs(delta[2]);
  const label = mode === "move" ? `Moved ${who} ${formatLength(distance, project.units)}` : mode === "copy" ? `Copied ${who}` : `Mirror-copied ${who}`;
  return { project: next, label };
}
```

(Copy names: the engine requires unique part names, not instance names; a copy shares its part, so "Top copy" is only the placed copy's name. `distance` sums the axes, which is exact because a move is along one axis (`lockedDelta`); a move with more than one non-zero axis comes only from code, never from the tool: assert that in the tool, not here.)

The tool, in the same file:

```ts
/** The placed boards the user has selected, as the engine last placed them. */
function selectedBoards(state: KerfState): PlacedPart[] {
  const sel = state.selection;
  const placed = state.solved?.result.placements ?? [];
  return sel.kind === "boards" ? placed.filter((p) => sel.instances.includes(p.instance)) : [];
}

const modeOf = (e: { ctrl: boolean; shift: boolean }): MoveMode => (e.ctrl ? "copy" : e.shift ? "mirror" : "move");

/** Drag the selected boards one direction at a time, or type a distance along the locked axis. */
export const moveTool: ToolHandler = {
  pointer(e, store) {
    const s = store.getState();
    const h = s.history;
    const project = h?.entries[h.at]?.project;
    if (!project) return;
    const pending = s.pending?.tool === "move" ? s.pending : null;
    if (e.kind === "down" && e.hit) {
      const chosen = selectedBoards(s);
      s.setPending({ tool: "move", from: e.hit.point, placed: chosen.length > 0 ? chosen : [e.hit.placed], axis: pending?.axis ?? null, last: null });
      return;
    }
    if (!pending || !e.hit || pending.placed.length === 0) return;
    const edit = moveBoards(project, pending.placed, lockedDelta(pending.from, e.hit.point, pending.axis), modeOf(e));
    if (e.kind === "move") {
      s.preview(edit.project);
      s.setPending({ ...pending, last: edit.label });
    }
    if (e.kind === "up" && pending.last) {
      s.commit(pending.last);
      s.setPending(null);
    }
  },
  prompt: (s) => (selectedBoards(s).length === 0 ? "Select boards first" : 'Distance, e.g. 2"'),
  measure(text, store) {
    const s = store.getState();
    const h = s.history;
    const project = h?.entries[h.at]?.project;
    const boards = selectedBoards(s);
    if (!project || boards.length === 0) return "Select the boards to move first.";
    const read = readLength(text, project);
    if (!read.ok) return read.message;
    const axis = s.pending?.tool === "move" ? (s.pending.axis ?? 0) : 0;
    const delta = [0, 1, 2].map((i) => (i === axis ? read.value : 0)) as unknown as Vec3;
    s.apply(moveBoards(project, boards, delta, "move"));
    s.setPending(null);
    return null;
  },
};
```

Imports: `readLength`, `type KerfState`, `type ToolHandler`. The `Pending` move member becomes `{ tool: "move"; from: Vec3; placed: PlacedPart[]; axis: 0 | 1 | 2 | null; last: string | null }`. Arrow keys lock the axis (SketchUp's red, green, blue). In `useShortcuts`, before the tool keys:

```ts
      const AXIS_KEYS: Record<string, 0 | 1 | 2> = { ArrowRight: 0, ArrowLeft: 1, ArrowUp: 2 };
      const axis = AXIS_KEYS[e.key];
      if (s.tool === "move" && axis !== undefined) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const pending = s.pending?.tool === "move" ? s.pending : { tool: "move" as const, from: [0, 0, 0] as Vec3, placed: [], axis, last: null };
        s.setPending({ ...pending, axis });
        return;
      }
```

Set `HANDLERS.move = moveTool`. A typed move goes along the locked axis (ArrowUp: up), which is what the end-to-end test above does.

```ts
describe("orientationFor()", () => {
  it("gives 0 for length along +x and width along +y", () => {
    expect(orientationFor([1, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0])).toBe(0);
```

```ts
import { solve } from "@kerf/core";

    expect(label).toBe("Added Board 1");

    expect(b?.box).toEqual({ min: [0, 0, 0], max: [24 * IN, 6 * IN, 146_050] });
  });
});

describe("boardBetween()", () => {
  it("fits the gap between the sides: 34-9/16 at 36 wide", () => {
    const p = base();
    const id = (n: string) => p.instances.find((i) => i.name === n)?.id ?? "";
    const r = boardBetween(p, STOCK_IDS.plywood34, id("Right side"), id("Left side"), 36 * IN, 11 * IN);
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.message);
    expect(solve(r.edit.project, shop).placements.find((x) => x.name === "Board 1")?.size.length).toBe(7_023_100);
  });

  it("follows the width: 28-9/16 at 30 wide", () => {
    const p = base();
    const id = (n: string) => p.instances.find((i) => i.name === n)?.id ?? "";
    const r = boardBetween(p, STOCK_IDS.plywood34, id("Right side"), id("Left side"), 36 * IN, 11 * IN);
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.message);
    const resized = withParam(r.edit.project, "width", "30in");
    expect(solve(resized, shop).placements.find((x) => x.name === "Board 1")?.size.length).toBe(5_803_900);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Core, in `solve/orientation.ts` (exported publicly through `api.ts` with its contract):

```ts
/**
 * The orientation index (ADR 003) whose length axis is `length` and width axis is `width`, both
 * signed unit axes of the parent frame at right angles. Throws RangeError otherwise.
 */
export function orientationFor(length: readonly number[], width: readonly number[]): number {
  for (let i = 0; i < ORIENTATION_COUNT; i += 1) {
    const o = orientationOf(i);
    if (o.x.every((n, k) => n === length[k]) && o.y.every((n, k) => n === width[k])) return i;
  }
  throw new RangeError("A board's length and width must lie along two different axes, at right angles.");
}
```

App `tools/board.ts`:

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Board (Interaction spec, "3D tools": "Draw a board from stock; drawn between two parts, its length
// binds to the gap"). Thickness is always the stock's; a board between two boards uses the engine's
// gap expression (ADR 015, rule 7), so it follows every resize.

import { gapBetween, type Id, type Len, orientationFor, type Project, type ShopProfile, solve, type Vec3 } from "@kerf/core";
import type { Edit, EditResult } from "../model/commands";
import { newId } from "../model/ids";
import { literalFor } from "../model/size";

const AXES = ["x", "y", "z"] as const;
const unit = (axis: 0 | 1 | 2): Vec3 => [axis === 0 ? 1 : 0, axis === 1 ? 1 : 0, axis === 2 ? 1 : 0];
/** Placements do not depend on the shop; an empty one is enough to place boards. */
const NO_SHOP: ShopProfile = { kerf: 0, tools: [] };

/** "Board 1", "Board 2" …: the next unused name (part names must be unique). */
function nextBoardName(project: Project): string {
  const used = new Set(project.parts.map((p) => p.name));
  let n = 1;
  while (used.has(`Board ${n}`)) n += 1;
  return `Board ${n}`;
}

function addBoard(project: Project, stock: Id, length: string, width: string, position: Record<"x" | "y" | "z", string>, orientation: number): { project: Project; name: string } {
  const name = nextBoardName(project);
  const part = { id: newId(), name, stock, length, width, grainLocked: false, ops: [] };
  const instance = { id: newId(), def: part.id, name, position, orientation, mirrored: false };
  return { project: { ...project, parts: [...project.parts, part], instances: [...project.instances, instance] }, name };
}

/** A board with its lowest corner at `corner`, its length and width along two axes. */
export function drawBoard(project: Project, stock: Id, corner: Vec3, length: { axis: 0 | 1 | 2; size: Len }, width: { axis: 0 | 1 | 2; size: Len }): Edit {
  const position = { x: literalFor(corner[0], project.units), y: literalFor(corner[1], project.units), z: literalFor(corner[2], project.units) };
  const made = addBoard(project, stock, literalFor(length.size, project.units), literalFor(width.size, project.units), position, orientationFor(unit(length.axis), unit(width.axis)));
  return { project: made.project, label: `Added ${made.name}` };
}

/** A board spanning the gap between two boards, lying flat at `height`, `widthSize` wide. */
export function boardBetween(project: Project, stock: Id, a: Id, b: Id, height: Len, widthSize: Len): EditResult {
  const gap = gapBetween(project, a, b);
  if (!gap.ok) return { ok: false, message: gap.reason };
  const lengthAxis = AXES.indexOf(gap.axis) as 0 | 1 | 2;
  if (lengthAxis === 2) return { ok: false, message: "A board between two boards one above the other is not supported yet; draw it on the lower board." };
  const widthAxis = (lengthAxis === 0 ? 1 : 0) as 0 | 1;
  // Line the new board up with the backmost of the two boards across the gap (placements are the
  // engine's; the gap expression itself carries the length).
  const placed = solve(project, NO_SHOP).placements.filter((p) => p.instance === a || p.instance === b);
  const across = Math.max(...placed.map((p) => p.box.min[widthAxis]));
  const position = { x: "0in", y: "0in", z: literalFor(height, project.units) };
  position[AXES[widthAxis]] = literalFor(across, project.units);
  position[gap.axis] = gap.start;
  const made = addBoard(project, stock, gap.length, literalFor(widthSize, project.units), position, orientationFor(unit(lengthAxis), unit(widthAxis)));
  const names = [a, b].map((id) => project.instances.find((i) => i.id === id)?.name ?? "a board");
  return { ok: true, edit: { project: made.project, label: `Added ${made.name} between ${names[0]} and ${names[1]}` } };
}
```
