The tool, in the same file. It draws in the stock chosen in the inspector's "Stock" choice while the Board tool is active: a store field `boardStock: Id | null` (default: the project's first sheet stock), shown by a `BoardStockPanel` that the Inspector renders when `tool === "board"`, holding one `Choice` labelled "Stock" over `project.stock`.

```ts
/** A board, then a second board (the new board fills the gap), or a point and typed sizes. */
export const boardTool: ToolHandler = {
  pointer(e, store) {
    if (e.kind !== "down") return;
    const s = store.getState();
    const project = shownProject(s);
    if (!project) return;
    const stock = s.boardStock ?? project.stock.find((x) => x.kind === "sheet")?.id;
    if (!stock) {
      s.say("Add a stock to the project first.");
      return;
    }
    const first = s.pending?.tool === "board" ? s.pending.from : null;
    if (first?.placed && e.hit && e.hit.placed.instance !== first.placed.instance) {
      const a = first.placed;
      const b = e.hit.placed;
      const widthAxis = Math.abs(a.frame.y[0]) === 1 ? 0 : 1;
      const width = Math.min(a.box.max[widthAxis] - a.box.min[widthAxis], b.box.max[widthAxis] - b.box.min[widthAxis]);
      const r = boardBetween(project, stock, a.instance, b.instance, first.point[2], width);
      if (r.ok) s.apply(r.edit);
      else s.say(r.message);
      s.setPending(null);
      return;
    }
    const point = e.hit?.point ?? e.ground;
    if (point) s.setPending({ tool: "board", from: { placed: e.hit?.placed ?? null, point } });
  },
  prompt: (s) => (s.pending?.tool === "board" ? 'Length, width (e.g. 24", 6"), or click a second board' : "Click a board or the floor"),
  measure(text, store) {
    const s = store.getState();
    const project = shownProject(s);
    const pending = s.pending?.tool === "board" ? s.pending : null;
    if (!project || !pending) return "Click where the board starts first.";
    const [l, w] = text.split(",").map((t) => readLength(t.trim(), project));
    if (!l?.ok || !w?.ok) return 'Type the length and width, like 24", 6".';
    const stock = s.boardStock ?? project.stock.find((x) => x.kind === "sheet")?.id;
    if (!stock) return "Add a stock to the project first.";
    s.apply(drawBoard(project, stock, pending.from.point, { axis: 0, size: l.value }, { axis: 1, size: w.value }));
    s.setPending(null);
    return null;
  },
};
```

The `Pending` board member becomes `{ tool: "board"; from: { placed: PlacedPart | null; point: Vec3 } }`, and `ModelPointer` gains `ground: Vec3 | null`, the floor point under the pointer. In `dispatchPointer` (Task 24), compute it for every event from the pointer's ray: three.js events carry `e.ray`; for `onPointerMissed` (a plain `MouseEvent`) build the ray with `new Raycaster().setFromCamera(ndc, camera)` from the pointer's normalized position and the Canvas camera, which the Canvas passes in through a `useThree` wrapper. Intersect with the floor plane `new Plane(new Vector3(0, 1, 0), 0)` and snap with `fromScene`. Free boards are drawn lying flat (length along x, width along y). Set `HANDLERS.board = boardTool`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `tools/join.ts` and `tools/JoinPanel.tsx`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Join (Interaction spec, "3D tools"): two clicks, a proposal from the engine (proposeJoint, ADR 015
// rule 6), then Accept or another type. Boards the form made are taken over first, because a
// hand-made joint may not name a generated board (ADR 014, "After review").

import { type Id, type Joint, type JointProposal, type PlacedPart, type Project, proposeJoint, type ShopProfile } from "@kerf/core";
import { detachForEdit, type EditResult, instanceOf } from "../model/commands";
import { newId } from "../model/ids";

export interface Proposal {
  a: Id;
  b: Id;
  result: JointProposal;
}

const WORD: Record<Joint["type"], string> = { dado: "a dado", rabbet: "a rabbet", throughDovetail: "through dovetails" };

export function propose(project: Project, shop: ShopProfile, a: PlacedPart, b: PlacedPart, prefer?: Joint["type"]): Proposal {
  return { a: a.instance, b: b.instance, result: proposeJoint(project, shop, a.instance, b.instance, newId(), prefer) };
}

export function acceptProposal(project: Project, shop: ShopProfile, a: PlacedPart, b: PlacedPart, type: Joint["type"]): EditResult {
  let next = detachForEdit(project, a).project;
  next = detachForEdit(next, b).project;
  const ia = instanceOf(next, a);
  const ib = instanceOf(next, b);
  if (!ia || !ib) return { ok: false, message: "One of the boards is no longer in the project." };
  const r = proposeJoint(next, shop, ia.id, ib.id, newId(), type);
  if (!r.ok) return { ok: false, message: r.reason };
  const host = r.joint.host === ia.id ? a : b;
  const guest = host === a ? b : a;
  return { ok: true, edit: { project: { ...next, joints: [...next.joints, r.joint] }, label: `Joined ${guest.name} to ${host.name} with ${WORD[type]}` } };
}
```

`tools/JoinPanel.tsx` (the Inspector shows it, before the step switch, when `tool === "join"`):

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The Join proposal: what the engine suggests, whether the shop can cut it, Accept, or another type.

import type { Joint } from "@kerf/core";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Section } from "../ui/Section";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "../ui/useKerf";
import { acceptProposal, propose } from "./join";

const OTHER: Partial<Record<Joint["type"], Joint["type"]>> = { dado: "rabbet", rabbet: "dado" };
const NAME: Record<Joint["type"], string> = { dado: "a dado", rabbet: "a rabbet", throughDovetail: "through dovetails" };

export function JoinPanel() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const proposal = useKerf((s) => s.proposal);
  if (!proposal) return <p className="empty">Click two touching boards.</p>;
  if (!proposal.result.ok) {
    return (
      <p role="note" className="empty">
        {proposal.result.reason}
      </p>
    );
  }
  const { joint, missing, description } = proposal.result;
  const boards = () => {
    const placed = store.getState().solved?.result.placements ?? [];
    return [placed.find((p) => p.instance === proposal.a), placed.find((p) => p.instance === proposal.b)] as const;
  };
  const accept = (): void => {
    const s = store.getState();
    const project = shownProject(s);
    const [a, b] = boards();
    if (!project || !a || !b) return;
    const r = acceptProposal(project, s.shop, a, b, joint.type);
    if (!r.ok) {
      s.say(r.message);
      return;
    }
    const tookOver = [a, b].filter((p) => p.source && !p.detached).map((p) => p.name);
    const notice = tookOver.length === 0 ? undefined : `${tookOver.join(" and ")} ${tookOver.length > 1 ? "are" : "is"} now custom; the form won't change ${tookOver.length > 1 ? "them" : "it"}.`;
    s.apply(r.edit, notice);
    s.setProposal(null);
  };
  const other = OTHER[joint.type];
  const tryOther = (): void => {
    const s = store.getState();
    const project = shownProject(s);
    const [a, b] = boards();
    if (project && a && b && other) s.setProposal(propose(project, s.shop, a, b, other));
  };
  return (
    <Section title="Proposed joint">
      <p>{description}</p>
      <p className="muted">{missing ? "Your shop has no tool for this cut yet." : "Your tools can cut this."}</p>
      <div className="button-row">
        <button type="button" className="primary" onClick={accept}>
          Accept
        </button>
        {other && (
          <button type="button" onClick={tryOther}>
            Use {NAME[other]} instead
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </Section>
  );
}
```

Store: `proposal: Proposal | null` (default null) and `setProposal(p)`; `setTool` clears it. The tool, in `join.ts`:

```ts
/** The first click picks a board; the second asks the engine for a joint between the two. */
export const joinTool: ToolHandler = {
  pointer(e, store) {
    if (e.kind !== "down" || !e.hit) return;
    const s = store.getState();
    const project = shownProject(s);
    if (!project) return;
    const firstId = s.pending?.tool === "join" ? s.pending.first : null;
    const first = s.solved?.result.placements.find((p) => p.instance === firstId);
    if (!first || first.instance === e.hit.placed.instance) {
      s.setPending({ tool: "join", first: e.hit.placed.instance });
      s.setProposal(null);
      return;
    }
    s.setProposal(propose(project, s.shop, first, e.hit.placed));
    s.setPending(null);
  },
  prompt: (s) => (s.pending?.tool === "join" ? "Click the second board" : "Click the first board"),
};
```

Set `HANDLERS.join = joinTool`. `tools/JoinPanel.test.tsx`: with a refused proposal in the store the panel shows the reason as a note; with the shelf proposal from `withShelf()` (above), clicking Accept records "Joined Board 1 to Right side with a dado".

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Interaction spec, "Tape: Measure, add guide lines". Lengths stay integers: the straight distance
// is the exact square root snapped once to the grid, half to even (Domain model, "Units & precision").

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { measureBetween } from "./tape";

const IN = 203_200;

describe("measureBetween()", () => {
  it("reads 36\" left to right", () => {
    expect(measureBetween([0, 0, 0], [36 * IN, 0, 0], "imperial")).toEqual({ leftRight: 36 * IN, frontBack: 0, upDown: 0, straight: 36 * IN });
  });

  it("reads a 3-4-5 triangle's long side as exactly 5\"", () => {
    expect(measureBetween([0, 0, 0], [3 * IN, 4 * IN, 0], "imperial").straight).toBe(5 * IN);
  });

  it("snaps a straight distance between grid lines to the nearest 1/64\"", () => {
    // √(1² + 1²)" = 1.41421…" = 90.51/64" → 91/64" = 288925 units.
    expect(measureBetween([0, 0, 0], [IN, IN, 0], "imperial").straight).toBe(91 * 3175);
  });

  it("gives distances, never negative", () => {
    expect(measureBetween([10 * IN, 0, 0], [0, 0, 0], "imperial").leftRight).toBe(10 * IN);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `tools/tape.ts`**

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Tape (Interaction spec, "3D tools": "Measure, add guide lines"). Distances along each direction
// are exact; the straight distance is the integer square root, snapped once to the grid. Guide
// lines live for the session and are never saved (ADR 015, D6).

import { gridSize, type Len, type Project, type Vec3 } from "@kerf/core";

export interface TapeReading {
  leftRight: Len;
  frontBack: Len;
  upDown: Len;
  straight: Len;
}

/** ⌊√n⌋ for a non-negative bigint (Newton's method; exact). */
function isqrt(n: bigint): bigint {
  if (n < 2n) return n;
  let x = n;
  let y = (x + 1n) / 2n;
  while (y < x) {
    x = y;
    y = (x + n / x) / 2n;
  }
  return x;
}

/** √n rounded to the nearest multiple of `grid`, half to even, in integer arithmetic. */
function snappedRoot(n: bigint, grid: bigint): bigint {
  const lower = isqrt(n) / grid; // whole grid steps at or below √n (⌊⌊√n⌋/g⌋ = ⌊√n/g⌋)
  // √n against the midpoint (lower + ½)·grid, compared squared: 4n against ((2·lower + 1)·grid)².
  const mid = ((2n * lower + 1n) * grid) ** 2n;
  if (4n * n > mid) return (lower + 1n) * grid;
  if (4n * n < mid) return lower * grid;
  return (lower % 2n === 0n ? lower : lower + 1n) * grid;
}

export function measureBetween(a: Vec3, b: Vec3, units: Project["units"]): TapeReading {
  const d = [0, 1, 2].map((i) => Math.abs((b[i] ?? 0) - (a[i] ?? 0)));
  const squares = d.reduce((sum, n) => sum + BigInt(n) * BigInt(n), 0n);
  return { leftRight: d[0] ?? 0, frontBack: d[1] ?? 0, upDown: d[2] ?? 0, straight: Number(snappedRoot(squares, BigInt(gridSize(units)))) };
}
```

Hand check for the third test: n = 2 × 203200² = 82,580,480,000; ⌊√n⌋ = 287,368; ⌊287,368 / 3175⌋ = 90; mid = (181 × 3175)² = 330,251,355,625; 4n = 330,321,920,000 > mid, so 91 steps = 288,925 units.

Store additions: `tape: { from: Vec3; to: Vec3 } | null`, `guides: Array<readonly [Vec3, Vec3]>`, `setTape(t)`, `addGuide(from, to)`, `clearGuides()`. Guides are never written to storage or the file (ADR 015, D6). The tool, in `tape.ts`:

```ts
/** Two clicks measure; the reading shows in the inspector. */
export const tapeTool: ToolHandler = {
  pointer(e, store) {
    if (e.kind !== "down") return;
    const point = e.hit?.point ?? e.ground;
    if (!point) return;
    const s = store.getState();
    if (s.pending?.tool === "tape") {
      s.setTape({ from: s.pending.from, to: point });
      s.setPending(null);
    } else {
      s.setTape(null);
      s.setPending({ tool: "tape", from: point });
    }
  },
  prompt: (s) => (s.pending?.tool === "tape" ? "Click the second point" : "Click the first point"),
};
```

`tools/TapePanel.tsx` (the Inspector shows it when `tool === "tape"`):

```tsx
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// The Tape reading and guide lines (Interaction spec, "Tape: Measure, add guide lines").

import { formatLength } from "@kerf/core";
import { shownProject } from "../model/store";
import { Section } from "../ui/Section";
import { useKerf, useKerfStore } from "../ui/useKerf";
import { measureBetween } from "./tape";

export function TapePanel() {
  const store = useKerfStore();
  const tape = useKerf((s) => s.tape);
  const units = useKerf((s) => shownProject(s)?.units ?? "imperial");
  const guides = useKerf((s) => s.guides.length);
  if (!tape) return <p className="empty">Click two points to measure.</p>;
  const r = measureBetween(tape.from, tape.to, units);
  const f = (n: number): string => formatLength(n, units);
  return (
    <Section title="Measured">
      <dl className="facts">
        <dt>Left to right</dt>
        <dd>{f(r.leftRight)}</dd>
        <dt>Front to back</dt>
        <dd>{f(r.frontBack)}</dd>
        <dt>Up and down</dt>
        <dd>{f(r.upDown)}</dd>
        <dt>Straight line</dt>
        <dd>{f(r.straight)}</dd>
      </dl>
      <div className="button-row">
        <button type="button" onClick={() => store.getState().addGuide(tape.from, tape.to)}>
          Keep as a guide line
        </button>
        {guides > 0 && (
          <button type="button" onClick={() => store.getState().clearGuides()}>
            Clear guide lines
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </Section>
  );
}
```

The viewport draws each guide as a dashed line (render `<Guides />` inside the Canvas):

```tsx
function Guides() {
  const guides = useKerf((s) => s.guides);
  const geometry = useMemo(() => {
    const g = new BufferGeometry();
    const points = guides.flatMap(([a, b]) => [...toScene(a), ...toScene(b)].map(INCHES));
    g.setAttribute("position", new BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(points), 3));
    return g;
  }, [guides]);
  if (guides.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <lineSegments geometry={geometry} onUpdate={(line) => line.computeLineDistances()}>
      <lineDashedMaterial color={token("--color-focus")} dashSize={1} gapSize={0.5} />
    </lineSegments>
  );
}
```

`e2e/timing.spec.ts`:

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// Build plan, M4 acceptance: "solve under 100 ms"; Charter question 3: resizing re-solves "in under
// 100 ms". Measured in the page, from committing the Width to the cut list showing the new size.

import { isMilestoneActive } from "@kerf/fixtures";
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";

test("a width change reaches the cut list in under 100 ms", async ({ page }) => {
  test.fail(!isMilestoneActive("M4"), "M4: the app is not built yet");
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "New" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Bookcase" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Cut list" }).click();
  const ms = await page.evaluate(async () => {
    const label = [...document.querySelectorAll("label")].find((l) => l.textContent === "Width");
    const input = label?.control as HTMLInputElement | null;
    if (!input) throw new Error("no Width field");
    const setValue = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLInputElement.prototype, "value")?.set;
    input.focus();
    setValue?.call(input, '30"');
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
    const start = performance.now();
    input.blur();
    const line = () => document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid="cutlist-line"]')[1]?.textContent ?? "";
    while (!line().includes('29-1/16"')) await new Promise((r) => requestAnimationFrame(r));
    return performance.now() - start;
  });
  expect(ms).toBeLessThan(100);
});
```

`e2e/wording.spec.ts`:

```ts
// Kerf — free, open-source woodworking design tool
// Copyright (C) 2026 Kerf contributors
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only

// CLAUDE.md: banned in UI: mesh, vertex, face (geometry), component, instance, material, extrude,
// boolean. Checked on every screen of the core flow and every tool panel.

import { isMilestoneActive } from "@kerf/fixtures";
import { expect, type Page, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { bannedWordsIn } from "./support/banned-words";

const shown = (page: Page) => page.locator("body").innerText();

test("no screen of the core flow shows a banned word", async ({ page }) => {
  test.fail(!isMilestoneActive("M4"), "M4: the app is not built yet");
  await page.goto("/");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "New" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Bookcase" }).click();
  const seen: string[] = [await shown(page)];
  for (const step of ["Shape", "Divide", "Joinery", "Build plan"]) {
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: step }).click();
    for (const tab of ["Shopping", "Cut list", "Sheets", "Setups", "Assembly"]) {
      await page.getByRole("tab", { name: tab }).click();
      seen.push(await shown(page));
    }
  }
  await page.getByTestId("part-group-shelves").click();
  for (const key of ["b", "p", "m", "j", "f", "t"]) {
    await page.keyboard.press(key);
    seen.push(await shown(page));
  }
  expect(bannedWordsIn(seen.join("\n"))).toEqual([]);
});
```
